## Task 5 (0b-1 + 0b-7): declare `credit-notes.cancel` and fix the test that masks it — **ONE commit**

**Today's defect.** `POST /credit-notes/{creditNote}/cancel` already carries `->middleware('can:credit-notes.cancel')` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Document/Presentation/routes.php:269-271`) and **no tenant has ever had the row**. Spatie 6.25 swallows `PermissionDoesNotExist` in `checkPermissionTo`, so **every caller including `admin` gets a silent 403**. The key survives only in the legacy `PermissionSeeder.php:72` — evidence it was lost in a seeder consolidation, which is why §4.9.3 restores it *before* Task 11 deletes that file.

**Why 0b-7 rides in this commit.** `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/RefundResidualTenantIsolationTest.php:135-142` currently does `Permission::firstOrCreate(['name' => 'credit-notes.cancel', …])` and grants it, with a comment mischaracterising a permission that **does not exist in the catalogue** as one *"admin doesn't include by default"*. Replacing the `firstOrCreate` with a plain lookup reds the suite until the key is declared — and a knowingly red commit is not independently mergeable. Landing both together means the suite is green on **every** merged commit while still proving the key exists.

**Files**

- Modify: `apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php` — `legacyPermissionNames()` and `legacyRolePermissionGrants()`
- Modify: `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/RefundResidualTenantIsolationTest.php:135-142`
- Create: `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/CreditNoteCancelPermissionTest.php`

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — declare the key.** In `legacyPermissionNames()`, beside its siblings (`credit-notes.view`, `.create`, `.post`), add `'credit-notes.cancel'`. In `legacyRolePermissionGrants()`, add it to the `manager` block on the line that already carries `'credit-notes.view', 'credit-notes.create', 'credit-notes.post'` (lane `:679`). **Do not touch `permissionNames()`, `rolePermissionGrants()`, `run()` or the marker branch** — `general_manager` inherits `manager`'s set through the lane's own `rolePermissionGrants()` derivation (lane `:101-102`), so declaring it once on `manager` gives it to both, exactly as `LegacyRoleBaseline::WAVE_0B_ADDITIONS` expects.

- [ ] **Step 2 — fix the masking test.**

~~~php
        // The two keys these routes require MUST already exist in the seeded
        // catalogue. A firstOrCreate here would mask a missing key: Spatie 6.25
        // swallows PermissionDoesNotExist in checkPermissionTo, so an undeclared
        // key silently 403s EVERY caller including admin — which is exactly what
        // `credit-notes.cancel` did until wave 0b declared it (spec 4.9.3).
        foreach (['credit-notes.cancel', 'invoices.cancel'] as $perm) {
            $permission = Permission::query()
                ->where('name', $perm)
                ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
                ->first();
            $this->assertNotNull(
                $permission,
                "permission '{$perm}' is not in the seeded catalogue — the route that requires it 403s every caller, admin included.",
            );
            $this->userA->givePermissionTo($permission);
        }
~~~

      `invoices.cancel` is already in the catalogue (`apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:176` at HEAD; lane `:256`), so it passes unchanged — this loop now proves both rather than creating both.

- [ ] **Step 3 — the route test, written out** (plan gate r2 **M2-5**; rev 2 described its three properties and supplied no class). `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/CreditNoteCancelPermissionTest.php`:

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature\Document;

use App\Modules\Company\Domain\Company;
use App\Modules\Company\Domain\Enums\CompanyStatus;
use App\Modules\Company\Domain\UserCompanyMembership;
use App\Modules\Document\Domain\Document;
use App\Modules\Document\Domain\Enums\DocumentStatus;
use App\Modules\Document\Domain\Enums\DocumentType;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\UserStatus;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\User;
use App\Modules\Partner\Domain\Partner;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Enums\SubscriptionPlan;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Enums\TenantStatus;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Tenant;
use Database\Seeders\RolesAndPermissionsSeeder;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;
use Tests\TestCase;

/**
 * 0b-1. POST /credit-notes/{creditNote}/cancel has carried
 * ->middleware('can:credit-notes.cancel') since it shipped, and no tenant has
 * ever had the row. Spatie 6.25 swallows PermissionDoesNotExist in
 * checkPermissionTo, so the route has silently 403'd EVERY caller including
 * admin (spec 4.9.3).
 *
 * Three properties, in the order they fail if the key is missing again: the key
 * EXISTS, a holder gets through and the ROW changes, a non-holder does not.
 */
final class CreditNoteCancelPermissionTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    private PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        // Resolved once, from the container, and held on the class — NOT the
        // app() helper the sibling fixture reaches for (CLAUDE.md rule 13).
        $this->permissionRegistrar = $this->app->make(PermissionRegistrar::class);
        // The catalogue used by case 1 is THIS seeder's output, on the DEFAULT
        // team. cancellableCreditNoteAndActor() re-seeds per tenant it creates.
        $this->seed(RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::class);
    }

    /**
     * The regression pin, and deliberately the FIRST case: if this one fails the
     * other two fail for a reason that looks like an authorization bug and is
     * not.
     */
    #[Test]
    public function a_freshly_seeded_tenant_has_the_key_in_its_catalogue(): void
    {
        $this->assertTrue(
            Permission::query()->where('name', 'credit-notes.cancel')->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')->exists(),
            "'credit-notes.cancel' is not in the seeded catalogue. The route requires it, and Spatie fails closed on a "
            .'key nobody holds — so POST /credit-notes/{creditNote}/cancel 403s EVERY caller, admin included.',
        );
    }

    #[Test]
    public function a_holder_can_cancel_and_the_row_actually_changes(): void
    {
        [$actor, $creditNote] = $this->cancellableCreditNoteAndActor(['credit-notes.cancel']);

        $response = $this->actingAsForTenant($actor)
            ->postJson("/api/v1/credit-notes/{$creditNote->id}/cancel", ['reason' => 'Customer returned the goods']);

        $response->assertOk();
        // The ROW, not the status code: a 200 from a route that silently no-ops
        // is the failure mode this whole wave is about.
        $this->assertDatabaseHas('documents', [
            'id' => $creditNote->id,
            'status' => DocumentStatus::Cancelled->value,
        ]);
    }

    #[Test]
    public function a_non_holder_is_refused_and_the_row_is_untouched(): void
    {
        [$actor, $creditNote] = $this->cancellableCreditNoteAndActor([]);
        $statusBefore = $creditNote->status;

        $response = $this->actingAsForTenant($actor)
            ->postJson("/api/v1/credit-notes/{$creditNote->id}/cancel", ['reason' => 'Customer returned the goods']);

        $response->assertForbidden();
        $this->assertDatabaseHas('documents', ['id' => $creditNote->id, 'status' => $statusBefore->value]);
    }

    /**
     * The tenant/company/actor/credit-note fixture, supplied in full (plan gate
     * r3 **M3-3**; rev 3 called it and defined nothing, so the class did not
     * compile). It is `CreditNoteTenantIsolationTest`'s own fixture, narrowed
     * to one tenant — copy the field lists from there rather than inventing a
     * third spelling: `lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/CreditNoteTenantIsolationTest.php:78-137,476-495,516-524`.
     *
     * @param  list<string>  $keys
     * @return array{0: User, 1: Document}
     */
    private function cancellableCreditNoteAndActor(array $keys): array
    {
        $tenant = Tenant::create([
            'name' => 'CN Cancel',
            'slug' => 'cn-cancel-'.Str::lower(Str::random(8)),
            'status' => TenantStatus::Active,
            'plan' => SubscriptionPlan::Professional,
        ]);

        $company = Company::create([
            'tenant_id' => $tenant->id,
            'name' => 'CN Cancel Co',
            'legal_name' => 'CN Cancel Co SARL',
            'tax_id' => 'TAX-CN-CANCEL',
            'country_code' => 'FR',
            'locale' => 'fr_FR',
            'timezone' => 'Europe/Paris',
            'currency' => 'EUR',
            'status' => CompanyStatus::Active,
        ]);

        $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($tenant->id);
        $this->seed(RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::class);

        $actor = User::create([
            'tenant_id' => $tenant->id,
            'name' => 'Cancel Actor',
            'email' => 'cancel-actor-'.Str::lower(Str::random(8)).'@example.com',
            'password' => 'password123',
            'status' => UserStatus::Active,
        ]);
        UserCompanyMembership::create([
            'user_id' => $actor->id,
            'company_id' => $company->id,
            'role' => 'manager',
        ]);

        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            // A LOOKUP, never firstOrCreate — creating the row here is the exact
            // defect Step 2 removes from RefundResidualTenantIsolationTest, and
            // it would also destroy this file's red: the route would pass
            // BECAUSE the test invented the key the seeder forgot.
            //
            // A key the seeded catalogue does not carry is therefore simply not
            // granted, and the "holder" reaches the route holding nothing. That
            // is the red, and it is the production defect exactly: before
            // Step 1, `credit-notes.cancel` is absent, the grant is skipped,
            // and `assertOk()` fails with 403.
            $permission = Permission::query()
                ->where('name', $key)
                ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
                ->first();

            if ($permission !== null) {
                $actor->givePermissionTo($permission);
            }
        }

        // DRAFT, not Posted. RefundService::cancelCreditNote routes a POSTED
        // credit note through DocumentPostingService::cancel(), which needs an
        // OPEN VAT period and emits GL reversals — a fixture that can 422 for a
        // reason this test is not about (see
        // tests/Feature/Document/CancelRefusedOnNonOpenVatPeriodTest.php). A
        // draft takes the simple branch at
        // `lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/api/app/Modules/Document/Domain/Services/RefundService.php:862-873`,
        // which is the branch that writes DocumentStatus::Cancelled — the row
        // change this test asserts. The helper is named for what it guarantees,
        // "cancellable", not for a status the assertion never needs.
        $creditNote = Document::create([
            'tenant_id' => $tenant->id,
            'company_id' => $company->id,
            'partner_id' => Partner::create([
                'tenant_id' => $tenant->id,
                'company_id' => $company->id,
                'name' => 'CN Cancel Customer',
                'type' => 'customer',
            ])->id,
            'type' => DocumentType::CreditNote,
            'status' => DocumentStatus::Draft,
            'document_number' => null,
            'document_date' => now(),
            'currency' => $company->currency,
            'subtotal' => '100.000',
            'tax_amount' => '0.000',
            'total' => '100.000',
        ]);

        return [$actor, $creditNote];
    }

    private function actingAsForTenant(User $user): self
    {
        $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($user->tenant_id);

        /** @var self */
        return $this->actingAs($user, 'sanctum');
    }
}
~~~

      **Three corrections rev 4 makes to this recipe** (plan gate r3 **M3-3**, plus one the gate did not raise):

      1. **The helper is supplied, and it is a lookup-and-attach, never a create.** Gate r3 read rev 3's prose as requiring the helper to *assert* the permission exists before creating the holder, and correctly observed that such an assertion cannot coexist with the stated red. It does not assert: a missing key is skipped, the "holder" reaches the route with nothing, and the route 403s. The key is created **in the test's setup — by the seeder** (`setUp()`'s `$this->seed(RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::class)`), which is precisely why the red before Step 1 *is* "the holder reaches the route and receives 403 because the seeder lacks the key". `a_freshly_seeded_tenant_has_the_key_in_its_catalogue` is the case that pins the absence by name, so nothing is hidden by the skip.
      2. **`postedCreditNoteAndActor` is renamed `cancellableCreditNoteAndActor` and builds a DRAFT.** A *posted* credit note routes through `DocumentPostingService::cancel()` and can 422 on a non-open VAT period — a fixture failure that reads exactly like an authorization failure. The draft branch is the one that writes `DocumentStatus::Cancelled`, which is the row change the test asserts.
      3. **The request carries `reason` — rev 3's did not, and would have 422'd** (this is NOT a gate-r3 finding; found while supplying the body). `RefundController::cancelCreditNote` validates `['reason' => 'required|string|max:500']` at `lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/api/app/Modules/Document/Presentation/Controllers/RefundController.php:233-235`, so `postJson(..., [])` returns **422**, not the `200` rev 3 asserted. The non-holder case sends it too, so the two arms differ in **one** variable — the grant — which is the whole design of an authorization test. (`can:` runs before validation, so that arm is 403 either way; sending it anyway is what keeps the pair honest.)

      **The imports this class needs** — `App\Modules\Company\Domain\Company`, `…\Company\Domain\Enums\CompanyStatus`, `…\Company\Domain\UserCompanyMembership`, `…\Document\Domain\Document`, `…\Document\Domain\Enums\DocumentStatus`, `…\Document\Domain\Enums\DocumentType`, `…\Identity\Domain\Enums\UserStatus`, `…\Identity\Domain\User`, `…\Partner\Domain\Partner`, `…\Tenant\Domain\Enums\SubscriptionPlan`, `…\Tenant\Domain\Enums\TenantStatus`, `…\Tenant\Domain\Tenant`, `Database\Seeders\RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`, `Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase`, `Illuminate\Support\Str`, `PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test`, `Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission`, `Tests\TestCase` — are the sibling's list minus the ones only its SQL-log assertions need. **`PermissionRegistrar` is declared as a mutable `private PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar` and resolved once in `setUp()`** — `$this->permissionRegistrar = $this->app->make(PermissionRegistrar::class)`. *(Plan gate r4 **minor 3**: rev 4's prose called this "constructor-injected as `private readonly`" while the body it supplies — correctly — does not, because a PHPUnit `TestCase` does not own its constructor and a `readonly` property cannot be written in `setUp()`. The implementation is right; the description was not, and the description is what an executing agent would have tried to satisfy.)* It is resolved **from the container**, never via the `app()` helper; the sibling uses the `app()` helper at `:113-118`, which CLAUDE.md rule 13 forbids in new code and which this file does not copy.

      **Red first:** run the file **before** Step 1's seeder edit. `a_freshly_seeded_tenant_has_the_key_in_its_catalogue` fails on its own message; `a_holder_can_cancel…` fails with **403** — a "holder" refused because the key the grant loop looked for is not in the seeded catalogue, which is exactly the production defect, reproduced. `a_non_holder_is_refused…` is **green from the start**, and that is correct: it pins the negative half, which was never broken. Capture all three verbatim.

- [ ] **Step 4 — nothing in `Wave0bAdditionConstantTest` is affected, and nothing is left red.** That file's cases are pure constant arithmetic and were green from Task 1 (plan gate r1 **B0b-2**; rev 1 carried a knowingly-red catalogue assertion through Tasks 1–6 here). The catalogue-facing property — *every one of the nineteen is declared in the seeder* — ships as `Wave0bAdditionCatalogueTest` in **Task 6 Step 4**, the commit that declares the last of them. This task declares one of the nineteen and leaves the suite green.

      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b/apps/api && ./vendor/bin/phpstan analyse --level=8 database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php tests/Feature/Document/CreditNoteCancelPermissionTest.php
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b/apps/api && ./vendor/bin/pint --test database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php tests/Feature/Document
      ```

## Task 6 (0b-2/0b-3/0b-4): declare the remaining eighteen keys and gate their routes

**The nineteen keys, authoritative.** Seven go to `manager` (and therefore to `general_manager`, which derives from it); twelve are `admin`-only. See **Q-0b-2** and **Q-0b-3** for the two places the spec did not name the verbs and what this plan chose.

| # | Key | Template defaults | Routes it gates |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `credit-notes.cancel` | `manager` (Task 5) | `POST /credit-notes/{creditNote}/cancel` (gate already present) |
| 2 | `services.view` | `manager` | `GET /services`, `GET /services/{service}` |
| 3 | `services.create` | `manager` | `POST /services` |
| 4 | `services.update` | `manager` | `PATCH /services/{service}`, `DELETE /services/{service}` (Q-0b-2) |
| 5 | `service-categories.view` | `manager` | `GET /service-categories`, `/tree`, `/{category}` |
| 6 | `service-categories.create` | `manager` | `POST /service-categories` |
| 7 | `service-categories.update` | `manager` | `PATCH /service-categories/{category}`, `DELETE /service-categories/{category}` |
| 8 | `channels.view` | *admin only* | `GET /channels`, `GET /channels/orders`, `GET /channels/{id}/sync-operations`, `GET /channels/{id}/orders` |
| 9 | `channels.create` | *admin only* | `POST /channels` |
| 10 | `channels.update` | *admin only* | `POST /channels/{id}/test-connection`, `POST /channels/{id}/resync` |
| 11 | `channels.operate` | *admin only* | `POST /channels/{channelId}/orders/{orderId}/promote`, `.../products/{productId}/publish`, `.../unpublish` |
| 12 | `categories.view` | *admin only* | `GET /categories`, `/tree`, `/{id}` |
| 13 | `categories.create` | *admin only* | `POST /categories` |
| 14 | `categories.update` | *admin only* | `PUT /categories/{id}`, `POST /categories/reorder` |
| 15 | `categories.delete` | *admin only* | `DELETE /categories/{id}` |
| 16 | `progression.view` | *admin only* | `GET progression/profile`, `/milestones`, `/modules`, `/recommendations` |
| 17 | `progression.manage` | *admin only* | `POST progression/register`, `/modules/{moduleId}/activate`, `/recommendations/{id}/accept`, `/dismiss` |
| 18 | `purchase-hub.orders.create` | *admin only* | `POST purchase-hub/orders` |
| 19 | `companies.create` | *admin only* | `POST /companies` |

> **`POST /api/v1/companies` is the sharpest one.** Today `CreateCompanyRequest::authorize()` returns `true` and the controller then self-grants `MembershipRole::Owner` to the caller (`apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Presentation/Controllers/CompanyController.php:148-155`), so **any** authenticated user in **any** of the seeded roles can create a company and become its owner. It gains `can:companies.create`, `admin`-only.
>
> **The Channel webhook ingress group is NOT touched.** `Route::prefix('api/v1/webhooks/channels')` is deliberately unauthenticated — external platforms call it and it is protected by `Str::isUuid()` plus `throttle:channel-webhook`. It is in the **public** allow-list, not the uncovered count. Adding `can:` there would break every channel integration.
>
> **`progression.manage` and the `manage` invariant.** §4.2.2's rule is *"a resource may declare `Manage` **or** any of `Create`/`Update`/`Delete`, not both"*. `progression.*` declares `view` + `manage` and no CRUD verb, so it satisfies the rule as written. Wave 1's `PermissionRegistryConsistencyTest::manage_is_never_mixed_with_crud_on_one_resource` must stay green on it — check that when writing the `ProgressionPermissionManifest`.

---

### The frontend half of this task, which rev 1 did not have (plan gate r1 **BLOCKER B0b-4**)

Rev 1's Step 3 changed **one** FE guard — `services.edit` → `services.update` — and nothing else, while the task adds admin-only gates to Channels, Categories, Progression and company creation. Every one of those has a live caller that a non-admin can reach today, so shipping the backend half alone means a manager's page fires a request the code already knows will be refused. The plan even *accepted* that (*"Categories create is 403 with the standard envelope"*), which contradicts this plan's own reviewer gate — *"a fifth caller anywhere in `apps/web/src` or `apps/pos/src` is a blocker… a page that fires a request it knows will be refused is the defect this task exists to close"*. **Rev 2 guards every affected caller.**

**The census is a SCRIPT and its output, not a hand-written table (plan gate r4 BLOCKER B4-1).** Rev 4 asserted that its twenty-seven hand-counted rows were exhaustive. They were not: gate r4 found four live surfaces it had missed by reading the tree itself, and a mechanical sweep finds **eight more that the gate did not raise either**. A census that a human types is a census that is wrong by however many rows the typist did not think of, and *"every affected frontend caller is guarded"* is this task's central claim — so rev 5 stops asserting it and starts **computing** it.

`census_0b.py` walks every production `.ts`/`.tsx` under `apps/web/src` and prints every call site whose request path matches a wave-0b-gated endpoint, plus every `<Link to>` / `navigate()` / Sidebar `href:` / `<Route path>` that targets a wave-0b-gated SPA route, each with the key it needs **and the guard Task 6 applies to it**. It is not a grep: it reads string literals with a scanner that tracks `${…}` nesting (so `` `/services${q ? `?${q}` : ''}` `` is classified, where a regex terminates at the inner backtick and misses it), it substitutes module-level path constants (so `progressionApi`'s eight `` `${BASE}/…` `` calls resolve), and it **walks the `<Route>` tree** in `routes/index.tsx` so that the three different `path="categories"` routes — under `inventory`, under `services` and under `expenses` — are told apart by their **full** path rather than collapsed by their segment. The `expenses/categories` one is gated on `expense-categories.view` and is correctly **not** in the output; a segment match would have dragged it in.

The `GUARDS` map at the top is the self-check: every file the two tables touch must have an entry, and any file that does not prints `UNASSIGNED — census hole, stop and record it`. **An `UNASSIGNED` line, or a non-empty table C, is a blocker for this task, not a note.**

**Run it before Step 1, from the merged tree, read-only:**

```
mkdir -p /tmp/rbac-0b-census && cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b && python3 scripts/census_0b.py apps/web/src | tee /tmp/rbac-0b-census/census.md
```

The script ships in this plan (below) and is copied to `apps/erp/scripts/census_0b.py` as part of Task 6's staging, so the reviewer can re-run it and diff the output against the table pasted here. **Re-run it again after Step 3 and after Step 9's `git add`**: the second run's `GUARDS` map is what proves every row was actually edited, and the third catches a row that a merge re-introduced.

~~~python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Wave 0b frontend caller census — MECHANICAL and EXHAUSTIVE.

Usage:  python3 census_0b.py <path-to-apps/web/src>

Source of truth is the lane tree, exported read-only:
    git archive lane/w-lot-a-1a apps/web/src | tar -x -C <scratchpad>/lane-web

PRODUCTION FILES ONLY. Excluded: any path segment named __tests__, __mocks__,
__fixtures__, e2e or fixtures; the shared harness dir src/test/; and any file
matching *.test.[jt]sx? / *.spec.[jt]sx?.

Emits four tables:
  A. API call sites whose request path matches a wave-0b-gated endpoint.
  B. Navigation sites (<Link to>, navigate(), Sidebar href:, <Route path>)
     targeting a wave-0b-gated SPA route.
  C. UNRESOLVED — call sites in a wave-0b feature directory whose path
     argument is not a string literal. The script cannot classify these; a
     human must. (Empty is the expected state.)
  D. Per-file roll-up, which is the staging manifest.

Every row in A and B must carry a guard in Task 6:
  * a query row      -> the `enabled` flag on the view key
  * a mutate row     -> the control that triggers it, on the mutate key
  * a navigation row -> the link/route on that route's key
"""
import os
import re
import sys

ROOT = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'apps/web/src'

EXCLUDE_SEG = {'__tests__', '__mocks__', '__fixtures__', 'e2e', 'fixtures'}
EXCLUDE_FILE = re.compile(r'\.(test|spec)\.[jt]sx?$')

# Directories whose unresolved call sites are in wave-0b scope (table C).
SCOPE_DIRS = (
    'features/services', 'features/channels', 'features/categories',
    'features/catalog', 'features/progression', 'features/company',
    'features/batches', 'features/purchase-hub', 'features/documents',
    'components/molecules/pickers', 'components/organisms/CompanySelector',
    'components/organisms/AddCompanyModal', 'components/organisms/Sidebar',
)

# ── A. gated API endpoints: (path regex, verbs, required key) ────────────────
# Order matters: the first matching rule wins, so the more specific prefix
# (/services/categories, /service-categories) precedes /services.
API_RULES = [
    (r'^/services/categories(?:[/*]|$)',   {'GET'},                    'service-categories.view'),
    (r'^/services/categories(?:[/*]|$)',   {'POST'},                   'service-categories.create'),
    (r'^/services/categories(?:[/*]|$)',   {'PUT','PATCH','DELETE'},   'service-categories.update'),
    (r'^/service-categories(?:[/*]|$)',    {'GET'},                    'service-categories.view'),
    (r'^/service-categories(?:[/*]|$)',    {'POST'},                   'service-categories.create'),
    (r'^/service-categories(?:[/*]|$)',    {'PUT','PATCH','DELETE'},   'service-categories.update'),
    (r'^/services(?:[/*]|$)',              {'GET'},                    'services.view'),
    (r'^/services(?:[/*]|$)',              {'POST'},                   'services.create'),
    (r'^/services(?:[/*]|$)',              {'PUT','PATCH','DELETE'},   'services.update'),
    (r'^/channels/[^/]+/orders/[^/]+/promote', {'POST'},            'channels.operate'),
    (r'^/channels/[^/]+/products/',     {'POST'},                   'channels.operate'),
    (r'^/channels/[^/]+/(?:test-connection|resync)', {'POST'},      'channels.update'),
    (r'^/channels(?:\*)?$',                {'POST'},                   'channels.create'),
    (r'^/channels(?:[/*]|$)',              {'GET'},                    'channels.view'),
    (r'^/categories(?:[/*]|$)',            {'GET'},                    'categories.view'),
    (r'^/categories/reorder',           {'POST'},                   'categories.update'),
    (r'^/categories(?:[/*]|$)',            {'POST'},                   'categories.create'),
    (r'^/categories(?:[/*]|$)',            {'PUT','PATCH'},            'categories.update'),
    (r'^/categories(?:[/*]|$)',            {'DELETE'},                 'categories.delete'),
    (r'^/progression(?:[/*]|$)',           {'GET'},                    'progression.view'),
    (r'^/progression(?:[/*]|$)',           {'POST','PUT','PATCH','DELETE'}, 'progression.manage'),
    (r'^/companies(?:\*)?$',                   {'POST'},                   'companies.create'),
    (r'^/purchase-hub/orders',          {'POST'},                   'purchase-hub.orders.create'),
    (r'^/credit-notes/[^/]+/cancel',    {'POST'},                   'credit-notes.cancel'),
    (r'^/batches/[^/]+/recall',         {'POST'},                   'batches.recall'),
    (r'^/batches(?:[/*]|$)',               {'DELETE'},                 'batches.delete'),
]

# ── B. gated SPA routes ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
NAV_RULES = [
    (r'^/services/categories(?:[/*]|$)',  'service-categories.view'),
    (r'^/services/new$',               'services.create'),
    (r'^/services/[^/]*/edit$',        'services.update'),
    (r'^/services(?:[/*]|$)',             'services.view'),
    (r'^/settings/services(?:[/*]|$)',    'services.view'),
    (r'^/channels(?:[/*]|$)',             'channels.view'),
    (r'^/ecommerce/orders(?:[/*]|$)',     'channels.view'),
    (r'^/inventory/categories(?:[/*]|$)', 'categories.view'),
    (r'^/categories(?:[/*]|$)',           'categories.view'),
    (r'^/growth(?:[/*]|$)',               'progression.view'),
    (r'^/company-onboarding(?:[/*]|$)',   'companies.create'),
]


# ── Guard assigned to each row by Task 6. Keyed by FILE; every file the two
#    tables touch must appear here, or the script prints UNASSIGNED and the
#    census has a hole. This is the self-check that rev 4's hand-written table
#    could not have.
GUARDS = {
 'components/molecules/pickers/ServicePicker.tsx':
   "Step 3(b): `enabled: … && hasPermission('services.view')` at :103",
 'components/organisms/CommandPalette/useCommandPalette.ts':
   "Step 3(g): `permission: 'categories.view'` on the nav-categories item at :73 (CommandItem already has `permission?: Permission` at :43)",
 'components/organisms/CompanySelector/CompanySelector.tsx':
   "Step 3(c): the Add-company button renders only for `companies.create`",
 'components/organisms/Sidebar/Sidebar.tsx':
   "Step 3(g): one `permission:` field per nav child at :148/:217/:276/:277 (:147 keeps `permission: 'services'`)",
 'features/batches/api/batches.ts':
   "ALREADY GUARDED by the lane: BatchDetailPage.tsx:78-79 gates the recall and delete controls on `batches.recall`/`batches.delete`. No edit; Task 7 gates the routes.",
 'features/catalog/api/categories.ts':
   "Step 3(b2): gated at the CALLERS of features/catalog/api/queries.ts — CategoryManagementPage.tsx:19, CategorySelect.tsx:38, ProductForm.tsx:191 — each passing `{ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') }`",
 'features/categories/api/categoriesApi.ts':
   "Step 3(b2): gated at the CALLERS of features/categories/hooks/useCategories.ts — CategoriesPage.tsx:27, CategoryForm.tsx:26, CategorySelector.tsx:47 — each passing `{ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') }`; the three mutations are gated by the controls in Step 3(c)",
 'features/channels/api.ts':
   "Step 3(b): `enabled: … && hasPermission('channels.view')` in ChannelListPage.tsx:24, ChannelCreateWizard.tsx:13-16 and useAggregateChannelOrders.ts:22. The four write functions have ZERO callers (recorded, not assumed).",
 'features/channels/pages/ChannelListPage.tsx':
   "Route guard: `/channels/new` carries `permission=\"channels.view\"` (Step 3(a)); the page itself is unreachable without the key",
 'features/company/api.ts':
   "Step 3(c): `createCompany()`'s only production caller is AddCompanyModal, whose COMPONENT is guarded on `companies.create` (ruling (A))",
 'features/inventory/pages/InventoryHubPage.tsx':
   "Step 3(g): `permissionModule: 'categories.view'` on the categories card at :41-46 (HubCardDef.permissionModule is ModuleKey at :28; the self-mapped row makes it legal)",
 'features/progression/api/progressionApi.ts':
   "Step 3(b): `enabled: … && hasPermission('progression.view')` in useCompanyProgression.ts:89,:104, useModuleReadiness.ts:25 and useRecommendations.ts:24; Step 3(c) gates the activate/accept/dismiss controls on `progression.manage`",
 'features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx':
   "Step 3(b) query `enabled` at :61; Step 3(c) controls at :186-202/:235-242/:262-270; Step 3(d) path fix at :58/:66/:79/:92",
 'features/services/ServiceDetailPage.tsx':
   "Route `/services/:id` is guarded `moduleKey=\"services\"` -> services.view (Step 3(a) leaves it); Step 3(b) adds `&& hasPermission('services.view')` to the detail query `enabled` at :57; Step 3(c) wraps the Edit link at :168-175 and the Delete button at :176-182 in `hasPermission('services.update') &&`",
 'features/services/ServiceForm.tsx':
   "Routes `/services/new` (services.create) and `/services/:id/edit` (services.update) guard reachability; Step 3(b) adds `&& hasPermission('service-categories.view')` at :75 and `&& hasPermission('services.view')` at :87; Step 3(d) path fix at :72 and **api.put -> api.patch at :142**",
 'features/services/ServiceListPage.tsx':
   "Route `/services` is guarded `moduleKey=\"services\"` -> services.view, which covers the list query at :55-64; Step 3(b) adds `&& hasPermission('service-categories.view')` to the filter query `enabled` at :52; Step 3(c) wraps BOTH Add-Service links at :122-128 and :225-231 in `hasPermission('services.create') &&`; Step 3(g) the Manage-Categories link at :116-121; Step 3(d) path fix at :49",
 'routes/index.tsx':
   "Step 3(a): one `permission=` attribute per gated route",
}

VERB = {'api.get':'GET','api.post':'POST','api.put':'PUT','api.patch':'PATCH',
        'api.delete':'DELETE','apiGet':'GET','apiPost':'POST','apiPut':'PUT',
        'apiPatch':'PATCH','apiDelete':'DELETE'}

CALL_HEAD = re.compile(
    r'\b(api\.(?:get|post|put|patch|delete)|apiGet|apiPost|apiPut|apiPatch|apiDelete)\b'
    r'\s*(?:<[^()]*>)?\s*\(\s*')
CONST_DECL = re.compile(r'^\s*(?:export\s+)?const\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\s*=\s*[\'"`](/[^\'"`]*)[\'"`]', re.M)
NAV_SITE = re.compile(
    r'(?:\bto=\{?[`\'"]|\bnavigate\(\s*[`\'"]|\bhref:\s*[`\'"]|\bpath=[`\'"])')


def read_literal(text, i):
    """If text[i] opens a string literal, return (raw_body, end_index); else (None, i).

    Template literals are scanned with `${...}` brace tracking, so a NESTED
    template (`/services${q ? `?${q}` : ''}`) does not terminate the scan early.
    """
    q = text[i]
    if q not in '`\'"':
        return None, i
    out, j = [], i + 1
    depth = 0
    while j < len(text):
        c = text[j]
        if c == '\\':
            out.append(text[j:j + 2]); j += 2; continue
        if q == '`' and c == '$' and j + 1 < len(text) and text[j + 1] == '{':
            depth += 1; out.append('${'); j += 2; continue
        if q == '`' and depth > 0:
            if c == '{':
                depth += 1
            elif c == '}':
                depth -= 1
            out.append(c); j += 1; continue
        if c == q:
            return ''.join(out), j + 1
        if c == '\n' and q != '`':
            return None, i
        out.append(c); j += 1
    return None, i


def resolve(raw, consts):
    """Substitute known module constants, blank every other `${…}`, drop the query."""
    def sub(m):
        return consts.get(m.group(1).strip(), '*')
    out = re.sub(r'\$\{([^{}]*)\}', sub, raw)
    while '${' in out:                       # nested / unresolvable remainder
        out = out[:out.index('${')] + '*'
    return out.split('?')[0]



# ── B2. nested <Route> resolver, for the router files ───────────────────────
# `routes/index.tsx` declares RELATIVE segments (`<Route path="categories">`
# inside `<Route path="expenses">`), so a bare segment match is wrong: three
# different `path="categories"` routes live under inventory, services and
# expenses. This walks the JSX <Route> tree and yields the FULL path of each
# route, which is the only way to tell them apart mechanically.

def route_tree(text):
    """Yield (line, full_path) for every <Route> in a router file."""
    events = []
    i = 0
    while True:
        i = text.find('<Route', i)
        if i < 0:
            break
        # scan the opening tag, ignoring '>' that appears inside {…} or "…"
        j, depth, quote = i + 6, 0, None
        while j < len(text):
            c = text[j]
            if quote is not None:
                if c == quote:
                    quote = None
            elif c in '"\'`':
                quote = c
            elif c == '{':
                depth += 1
            elif c == '}':
                depth -= 1
            elif depth == 0 and c == '>':
                break
            j += 1
        tag = text[i:j]
        m = re.search(r'path=(?:"([^"]*)"|\{\s*[\'"`]([^\'"`]*)[\'"`]\s*\})', tag)
        seg = (m.group(1) or m.group(2)) if m else ''
        events.append(('open', seg, text.count('\n', 0, i) + 1,
                       tag.rstrip().endswith('/')))
        i = j + 1
    closes = [('close', '', text.count('\n', 0, m.start()) + 1, False)
              for m in re.finditer(r'</Route>', text)]
    events = sorted(events + closes, key=lambda e: e[2])

    stack = []
    for kind, seg, line, self_closing in events:
        if kind == 'close':
            if stack:
                stack.pop()
            continue
        full = '/' + '/'.join(p for p in (stack + [seg]) if p)
        yield line, re.sub(r'/+', '/', full)
        if not self_closing:
            stack.append(seg)


def files():
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(ROOT):
        dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in EXCLUDE_SEG]
        rel_dir = os.path.relpath(dirpath, ROOT)
        if rel_dir == 'test' or rel_dir.startswith('test' + os.sep):
            continue
        for fn in sorted(filenames):
            if fn.endswith(('.ts', '.tsx')) and not EXCLUDE_FILE.search(fn):
                yield os.path.join(dirpath, fn)


api_rows, nav_rows, unresolved = [], [], []
for path in files():
    rel = os.path.relpath(path, ROOT).replace(os.sep, '/')
    text = open(path, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
    consts = {m.group(1): m.group(2) for m in CONST_DECL.finditer(text)}
    in_scope = any(rel.startswith(d) for d in SCOPE_DIRS)

    for m in CALL_HEAD.finditer(text):
        verb = VERB[m.group(1)]
        raw, _ = read_literal(text, m.end())
        line = text.count('\n', 0, m.start()) + 1
        if raw is None:
            if in_scope:
                unresolved.append((rel, line, verb, text[m.end():m.end() + 40].split(',')[0].strip()))
            continue
        probe = resolve(raw, consts)
        if not probe.startswith('/'):
            continue
        for pattern, verbs, key in API_RULES:
            if verb in verbs and re.match(pattern, probe):
                api_rows.append((rel, line, verb, raw, key)); break

    if rel.startswith('routes/'):
        for line, full in route_tree(text):
            for pattern, key in NAV_RULES:
                if re.match(pattern, full):
                    nav_rows.append((rel, line, full, key)); break

    for m in NAV_SITE.finditer(text):
        if rel.startswith('routes/') and text[m.start():m.end()].startswith('path='):
            continue          # handled by route_tree(), which resolves nesting
        raw, _ = read_literal(text, m.end() - 1)
        if raw is None:
            continue
        probe = resolve(raw, consts)
        if not probe.startswith('/'):
            continue
        line = text.count('\n', 0, m.start()) + 1
        for pattern, key in NAV_RULES:
            if re.match(pattern, probe):
                nav_rows.append((rel, line, probe, key)); break

api_rows.sort(); nav_rows.sort(); unresolved.sort()

def guard(rel):
    return GUARDS.get(rel, '**UNASSIGNED — census hole, stop and record it**')

print('## A. API call sites (%d)\n' % len(api_rows))
print('| # | file:line | verb | request path | required key | guard applied in Task 6 |')
print('|---:|---|---|---|---|---|')
for n, r in enumerate(api_rows, 1):
    print('| %d | `apps/web/src/%s:%d` | %s | `%s` | `%s` | %s |'
          % (n, r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], guard(r[0])))

print('\n## B. Navigation / route sites (%d)\n' % len(nav_rows))
print('| # | file:line | target path | required key | guard applied in Task 6 |')
print('|---:|---|---|---|---|')
for n, r in enumerate(nav_rows, 1):
    print('| %d | `apps/web/src/%s:%d` | `%s` | `%s` | %s |'
          % (n, r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], guard(r[0])))

print('\n## C. UNRESOLVED path arguments in wave-0b scope (%d)\n' % len(unresolved))
if unresolved:
    print('| file:line | verb | expression |')
    print('|---|---|---|')
    for r in unresolved:
        print('| `apps/web/src/%s:%d` | %s | `%s` |' % (r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3]))
else:
    print('None. Every call site in wave-0b scope has a statically resolvable path.')

per_file = {}
for r in api_rows:
    per_file.setdefault(r[0], [0, 0])[0] += 1
for r in nav_rows:
    per_file.setdefault(r[0], [0, 0])[1] += 1
print('\n## D. Per-file roll-up — the staging manifest (%d files)\n' % len(per_file))
print('| file | API rows | nav rows |')
print('|---|---:|---:|')
for f in sorted(per_file):
    print('| `apps/web/src/%s` | %d | %d |' % (f, per_file[f][0], per_file[f][1]))

missing = sorted({r[0] for r in api_rows + nav_rows} - set(GUARDS))
print('\nUNASSIGNED FILES: %s' % (', '.join(missing) if missing else 'none — every row has a guard.'))

print('\nTOTAL: %d rows (%d API + %d navigation) across %d files; %d unresolved.'
      % (len(api_rows) + len(nav_rows), len(api_rows), len(nav_rows), len(per_file), len(unresolved)))
~~~

| # | file:line | verb | request path | required key | guard applied in Task 6 |
|---:|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `apps/web/src/components/molecules/pickers/ServicePicker.tsx:107` | GET | `/services?${params.toString()}` | `services.view` | Step 3(b): `enabled: … && hasPermission('services.view')` at :103 |
| 2 | `apps/web/src/features/batches/api/batches.ts:97` | DELETE | `/batches/${uuid}` | `batches.delete` | ALREADY GUARDED by the lane: BatchDetailPage.tsx:78-79 gates the recall and delete controls on `batches.recall`/`batches.delete`. No edit; Task 7 gates the routes. |
| 3 | `apps/web/src/features/batches/api/batches.ts:104` | POST | `/batches/${uuid}/recall` | `batches.recall` | ALREADY GUARDED by the lane: BatchDetailPage.tsx:78-79 gates the recall and delete controls on `batches.recall`/`batches.delete`. No edit; Task 7 gates the routes. |
| 4 | `apps/web/src/features/catalog/api/categories.ts:37` | GET | `/categories` | `categories.view` | Step 3(b2): gated at the CALLERS of features/catalog/api/queries.ts — CategoryManagementPage.tsx:19, CategorySelect.tsx:38, ProductForm.tsx:191 — each passing `{ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') }` |
| 5 | `apps/web/src/features/catalog/api/categories.ts:44` | GET | `/categories/tree` | `categories.view` | Step 3(b2): gated at the CALLERS of features/catalog/api/queries.ts — CategoryManagementPage.tsx:19, CategorySelect.tsx:38, ProductForm.tsx:191 — each passing `{ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') }` |
| 6 | `apps/web/src/features/catalog/api/categories.ts:51` | GET | `/categories/${id}` | `categories.view` | Step 3(b2): gated at the CALLERS of features/catalog/api/queries.ts — CategoryManagementPage.tsx:19, CategorySelect.tsx:38, ProductForm.tsx:191 — each passing `{ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') }` |

**What the eighty-seven contain that the twenty-seven did not.** Rev 4's table was a table of *surfaces a human noticed*; this one is a table of *call sites the compiler can see*. The delta splits three ways:

| Class | Rows | Where they came from |

| The four the gate register names (**B4-1**) | A1 (`ServicePicker`), A39–A40 + B13 (`ServiceDetailPage`'s detail read, DELETE and edit link), B19–B20 (both `ServiceListPage` "Add Service" links) | read off the lane by the reviewer |
| **Eight the register did NOT raise** | A4–A17 (the `/categories` API modules and their **transitive** callers), B1 (`CommandPalette`), B9 (`InventoryHubPage`) | found only by the script |
| Rows rev 4 *did* have, now with an exact line and an exact guard | the rest | re-derived, not retyped |

**The eight the gate did not raise, stated plainly, because the largest of them is a design consequence and not a typo.** `categories.view` is **admin-only** (key 12 of the nineteen). Rev 4's ruling **(B)** gated `useCategoryTree()` at *two* callers — `CategoriesPage.tsx:27` and `CategoryForm.tsx:26` — and explicitly left the namesake in `features/catalog/api/queries.ts` alone, reasoning that *"gating inside the hook would put a `categories.view` check on a product-form dropdown by accident."* The script shows that is not an accident that gating would cause; it is a **consequence the backend gate already causes**, and rev 4 shipped it unguarded:

| Live caller of a `/categories` read | Reached from |
|---|---|
| `features/catalog/pages/CategoryManagementPage.tsx:19` | **no route** — the page is not mounted anywhere in `routes/index.tsx` (the `AddCompanyModal` shape: guarded, no production instantiation) |
| `components/catalog/CategorySelect.tsx:38` | `features/products/sections/ProductGeneralSection.tsx:127` |
| `features/inventory/ProductForm.tsx:191` | the inventory product form |
| `features/categories/components/CategorySelector.tsx:47` | `features/coupons/pages/CouponFormPage.tsx:328`, `features/promotions/pages/PromotionFormPage.tsx:318`, `features/inventory-counting/pages/CreateCountingPage.tsx:499` |
| `features/categories/components/CategoryForm.tsx:26` | the Categories page's own form (rev 4 had this one) |
| `features/categories/CategoriesPage.tsx:27` | the Categories page (rev 4 had this one) |

After this wave gates `GET /categories/tree`, a `manager` who opens the **product form**, the **coupon form**, the **promotion form** or **create-counting** fires a request that returns 403. That is the exact defect B0b-4 exists to close, in six more places than rev 4 counted. **All six are guarded here** — each caller passes `{ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') }` into its hook, the same shape ruling (B) already chose — and the **UX consequence is recorded, not hidden**: for a non-admin those four forms render their category picker **empty and disabled** rather than firing a refused request. Whether a category *picker* should really be admin-only is a **product** question this plan does not answer; it is filed as residual **R-0b-10** with the six call sites named, for owner ruling before the wave deploys. What is not negotiable is that the UI must not ask for something the server has already decided to refuse.

`CommandPalette`'s `nav-categories` item (`useCommandPalette.ts:73`) and `InventoryHubPage`'s categories card (`:41-46`) are the same defect in navigation form: both carry `moduleKey`/`permissionModule: 'inventory'` and would keep offering a route the wave now gates. Both take a one-field edit and **neither needs a type change** — `CommandItem` already declares `permission?: Permission` (`useCommandPalette.ts:43`), and `HubCardDef.permissionModule` is `ModuleKey` (`InventoryHubPage.tsx:28`), which the self-mapped `'categories.view'` row from ruling **(D)** makes legal.

**Two rows are already guarded and take NO edit**, which the script records rather than leaving a reader to wonder: `features/batches/api/batches.ts:97` and `:104` are the two writes Task 7 gates, and `lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/web/src/features/batches/pages/BatchDetailPage.tsx:78-79` already computes `canRecall`/`canDelete` from `hasPermission('batches.recall')` / `hasPermission('batches.delete')`. The lane did the frontend half of its own gate. Say so; do not re-do it.

**Table C is the script's honest blind spot, and it has exactly one entry.** `features/documents/DocumentForm.tsx:391` posts to a variable, `apiEndpoint`, which no static pass can resolve. Read it at the merge moment: if it can ever hold a wave-0b path, it is a census row and needs a guard; if it cannot, say so in the handback. **Do not leave it unread.**

### The frontend rulings — three rev 2 left open (plan gate r2 **M2-3**), and a fourth rev 3 left contradictory (plan gate r3 **M3-1**)

**(A) `AddCompanyModal` has no production trigger, so the COMPONENT is what is guarded.** Rev 2's census called it *"a second, independent create surface"* and instructed *"its trigger renders only for `companies.create`"* — an instruction that cannot be executed, because there is no trigger. Measured at `lane/w-lot-a-1a` `52f5ad796`, every reference to it in `apps/web/src`:

| Reference | Kind |
|---|---|
| `components/organisms/AddCompanyModal/AddCompanyModal.tsx:12,41` | the definition |
| `components/organisms/AddCompanyModal/index.ts:1` · `components/organisms/index.ts:6` · `features/company/AddCompanyModal.tsx:2` | three re-export barrels / a back-compat shim |
| `components/organisms/AddCompanyModal/__tests__/AddCompanyModal.ordering.test.tsx:8,48` · `features/settings/__tests__/TunisiaLocalization.test.tsx:4,81` | the **only** two `<AddCompanyModal … />` renderings, both in `__tests__` |

**No non-test file renders it.** The **RULING**: guard the component itself, and record the fact rather than invent a trigger. The component owns the `POST` — its `useMutation` at `:64-90` calls `createCompany()` from `features/company/api`, and `handleSubmit` at `:92-115` fires it — so a guard on the component is a guard on the write, wherever a future caller mounts it. Two lines, at the existing early return at `:123`:
```tsx
// AddCompanyModal.tsx — beside the existing `if (!isOpen) return null`
const { hasPermission } = usePermissions()
const canCreateCompanies = hasPermission('companies.create')

if (!isOpen) return null
if (!canCreateCompanies) return null
```
and, because a `return null` is invisible to anyone reading a submit handler, the submit button at **`:326-333`** also carries `disabled={!canCreateCompanies || mutation.isPending}` — its existing `disabled` is at `:330`. *(Rev 3 said `:382-389`; the file is 339 lines long, so that range does not exist — plan gate r3 minor 1. The component opens at `:41`, `useMutation` at `:64-90`, `handleSubmit` at `:92`, and `if (!isOpen) return null` at `:123`; those three are correct as rev 3 wrote them.)* Belt and braces is right here precisely *because* there is no live caller to observe: the next person to mount this component will not read this plan.

**Record it as a fact, not a guess.** The handback's frontend census names `AddCompanyModal` as **guarded, no production instantiation** — the same shape as `channels.create/update/operate`, `purchase-hub.orders.create` and `credit-notes.cancel`. And **do not delete it**: the two test files render it, and deleting dead UI is not this wave's job. It is recorded as residual **R-0b-9**.

**(B) `useCategoryTree()` is gated at its CALLERS, not inside the hook.** Rev 2 left this *"to handback-time choice"*. The **RULING** is the callers, and the measurement is why:

- `features/categories/hooks/useCategories.ts:108-117` exports `useCategoryTree()`. Its callers are **two**, not one: `CategoriesPage.tsx:27` — the surface this wave gates — **and `features/categories/components/CategoryForm.tsx:26`**, which uses the tree to populate its *parent category* dropdown and which rev 2's census never named.
- A **namesake** hook, `features/catalog/api/queries.ts:76`, is a different function with three further callers — `features/catalog/pages/CategoryManagementPage.tsx:19`, `components/catalog/CategorySelect.tsx:38` and `features/inventory/ProductForm.tsx:191`. None of them is in this wave's scope, and a `grep useCategoryTree` cannot tell the two apart.

Gating inside the hook would therefore put a `categories.view` check on a product-form dropdown by accident and would hide the decision from the surface that owns it. The hook takes an optional flag and each caller supplies its own:
```ts
// useCategories.ts:108 — the signature gains one optional, defaulted argument
export function useCategoryTree(options: { enabled?: boolean } = {}): UseQueryResult<CategoryApiResponse[]> {
  const tenantId = useAuthStore((s) => s.user?.tenant_id ?? null)
  const companyId = useCompanyStore((s) => s.currentCompanyId ?? null)
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: tenantScopedKey([...categoryKeys.tree()]),
    queryFn: fetchCategoryTree,
    enabled: (options.enabled ?? true) && !!tenantId && !!companyId,
    staleTime: 300000,
  })
}
```
```tsx
// CategoriesPage.tsx:27  and  CategoryForm.tsx:26 — both callers, both explicit
const { data: categoryTree = [], isLoading, error } = useCategoryTree({ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') })
```
The default is `true`, so no other consumer of this file changes behaviour, the query key is untouched (`audit-tanstack-keys.mjs` stays green) and `staleTime` is untouched. **Name both callers in the handback**, and say that the catalog namesake was found and deliberately left alone.

**(C) `RequirePermission` REDIRECTS; it does not render a denial surface.** This is not a gate finding — rev 3 adds it — but it invalidates two assertions rev 2 wrote, so it is ruled here. At `lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/web/src/features/auth/components/RequirePermission.tsx:65-80`:
```tsx
if (!hasAccess) {
  if (fallback) { return <>{fallback}</> }
  if (location.pathname !== '/dashboard') {
    return <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace state={{ permissionDenied: true, from: location }} />
  }
  return <PermissionDenied />
}
```
Two consequences, both binding on every guard this wave writes:

1. **An in-page control guard MUST pass `fallback={null}`.** Without it, a `manager` opening the Categories page would be *navigated away* instead of simply not seeing a button. The house precedent is `features/income/pages/IncomeListPage.tsx:49,109` (`<RequirePermission permission="income.create" fallback={null}>`). This wave's control guards use the `hasPermission(...) &&` / conditional-prop form instead, which has the same effect with no import — either is acceptable; a bare `<RequirePermission>` around a button is **not**.
2. **A deep-linked denial lands on `/dashboard`, not on `errors.permissionDenied`.** Nothing in `apps/web/src` reads `state.permissionDenied` — the grep returns only `RequirePermission.tsx` itself — so the visible outcome is a plain dashboard. Both Playwright specs must assert **that**, plus the absence of the request. Rev 2's *"renders the `RequirePermission` denial surface (`errors.permissionDenied`)"* would have failed on every route except `/dashboard` itself, and an executing agent would most likely have "fixed" it by weakening the assertion.

**(D) The navigation entries and the in-page link ARE guarded — and doing so forces three new `MODULE_PERMISSIONS` rows. RULED (plan gate r3 M3-1).** Rev 3 wrote an e2e contract asserting that a `manager` sees no Channels, Categories or Growth nav entry and that a services-capable role without `service-categories.view` sees neither the Service-categories nav entry nor the in-page link — and, three hundred lines earlier, instructed the executing agent **not to touch** `ServiceListPage.tsx:117` or `Sidebar.tsx:148`. Both statements cannot ship. The accepted requirement is that **every visible caller is guarded**, so the guards are what rev 4 adds; the assertions stay.

**What the Sidebar's gate actually is, measured at `lane/w-lot-a-1a` `52f5ad796`.** A nav item declares an optional `permission`, and `isNavItemVisible()` at `:451-467` refuses the item when `canAccessModule(permission)` is false — fail-closed on both the module and the permission axis, with the group itself dropped when all its children are gated away (`:474-484`). The field is typed **`ModuleKey`**, not `Permission` (`Sidebar.tsx:110,120`), and `ModuleKey = keyof typeof MODULE_PERMISSIONS` (`usePermissions.ts:182`). So:

> **`apps/web/src/hooks/usePermissions.ts` is NOT untouched by this task, and rev 3 said it was.** Rev 3's note read *"`usePermissions.ts` is expected UNTOUCHED … this task adds no new module key. If a guard turns out to need a new one, that is a finding — record it."* This is that finding, recorded and resolved rather than left for the executing agent to hit at `pnpm typecheck`: three nav rows need three **self-mapped** `MODULE_PERMISSIONS` entries, exactly the shape the file already uses for `'inventory.transfers.view'`, `'replenishment.view'`, `'deliveries.view'` and `'goods-receipt.create-standalone'`:
> ```ts
> // usePermissions.ts — beside the existing self-mapped rows. Wave 0b (0b-2/0b-4):
> // the Sidebar nav `permission` field is a MODULE key, so a raw backend
> // permission string is a compile error until it is mapped. Self-mapped, so the
> // nav entry is visible to exactly the holders of the key its route now checks.
> 'channels.view': ['channels.view'],
> 'categories.view': ['categories.view'],
> 'service-categories.view': ['service-categories.view'],
> ```
> `usePermissions.ts` **is** in `lane/w-lot-a-1a`'s diff, so re-read it at the merge moment before editing; the four self-mapped precedents above are all at `dev` and at the lane tip. `MODULE_PERMISSIONS` is `as const satisfies Record<string, readonly Permission[]>` (`:174`), so each value must be a key of the **regenerated** map — which is why Step 3(f)'s regeneration must precede this edit as well.

**The edits, with post-lane lines — five in rev 4, SEVEN in rev 5.** Nothing else in any of these files changes: not a `className`, not a `t()` key, not an `href`.

```tsx
// Sidebar.tsx:148 — census row 23. `services` (→ services.view) is the right
// check for reaching the Services AREA and the wrong one for the category
// catalogue; the sibling entry at :147 keeps `permission: 'services'`.
{ key: 'serviceCategories', href: '/services/categories', icon: FolderTree, module: 'Workshop', permission: 'service-categories.view' },

// Sidebar.tsx:276-277 — census rows 24 and 25. The `ecommerce` GROUP keeps

{ key: 'channels', href: '/channels', icon: Cable, permission: 'channels.view' },
{ key: 'channelOrders', href: '/ecommerce/orders', icon: ClipboardList, permission: 'channels.view' },

// Sidebar.tsx:217 — census row 26. Same shape; the `catalog` group at :212-216
// keeps module: 'Catalog' + permission: 'inventory'.
```

```tsx
// ServiceListPage.tsx:116-121 — census row 27. WRAP the existing <Link>
// byte-identically; `to="/services/categories"` is an SPA path and does NOT

{hasPermission('service-categories.view') && (
```

`ServiceListPage.tsx` does not import `usePermissions` today (its imports end at `:17`), so this adds one import and one `const { hasPermission } = usePermissions()` — the same two lines rows 21 and 22 already require of it for the query guard.

**Two nav claims in rev 3's e2e that are NOT implementable, corrected here rather than "implemented" into something invented:**

1. **There is no Growth navigation entry to hide.** `grep -n "growth" apps/web/src/components/organisms/Sidebar/Sidebar.tsx` returns **nothing**, and no file in `apps/web/src` links to `/growth` at all — the two Progression routes (`routes/index.tsx:3210-3227`) are reachable **only by deep link**. Rev 3's *"manager lacks … Growth … navigation"* asserts the absence of something that is absent for everybody. The e2e keeps the Growth **deep-link redirect** and the **request-absence** assertion, and records the no-nav fact; it does **not** add a nav entry in order to hide it.
2. **There is no company-create navigation entry either.** The only company-create affordance in the shell is `CompanySelector.tsx:113-120`'s "Add company" button — **census row 14, already guarded by this task** — and `/company-onboarding` is census row 13. "Company-creation navigation" in the e2e means row 14's button, and the spec says so in those words.

Both facts go in the handback beside the `AddCompanyModal` one: **guarded where a surface exists; recorded where it does not.**

**Three keys need no frontend work, and saying so is part of the census:**
- **`channels.create` / `channels.update` / `channels.operate`** — `createChannel`, `testConnection`, `resyncChannel` and `promoteChannelOrder` are defined in `features/channels/api.ts:20,24,28,41` and have **zero callers** in `apps/web/src`; `ChannelCreateWizard.tsx:49-51`'s "Test connection" button has **no `onClick`**. Only `GET /channels` and `GET /channels/orders` are live, which is why only `channels.view` appears in the table.
- **`purchase-hub.orders.create`** — `grep -rn "purchase-hub" apps/web/src apps/pos/src` returns **nothing** in either app.
- **`credit-notes.cancel`** — no caller either: `apps/web/src` posts to `/credit-notes` (create, `features/documents/CreateCreditNotePage.tsx:236`) but never to `/credit-notes/{id}/cancel`.

Each of those three is recorded in the handback as *gated, no live caller* — a fact the reviewer needs, and the reason the wave gates them anyway is that the route exists and the authority question is real.

### The `/service-categories` versus `/services/categories` mismatch — RULED

**The backend route is canonical. The frontend calls are wrong and are fixed here.** `apps/api/app/Modules/Service/Presentation/routes.php:38,41,44,47,50,53` defines `service-categories{,/tree,/{category}}` under the `api/v1` prefix at `:20`; **no route named `services/categories` exists**. Six production call sites in `apps/web/src` target the non-existent path:

| File:line | Call | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:58` | `api.get('/services/categories')` | `'/service-categories'` |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:66` | `api.post('/services/categories', data)` | `'/service-categories'` |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:79` | ``api.put(`/services/categories/${id}`, data)`` | ``api.patch(`/service-categories/${id}`, data)`` — the backend defines **PATCH** at `routes.php:50`, not PUT |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:92` | ``api.delete(`/services/categories/${id}`)`` | ``/service-categories/${id}`` |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceForm.tsx:72` | `api.get('/services/categories')` | `'/service-categories'` |
| **`apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceListPage.tsx:49`** | `api.get('/services/categories')` | `'/service-categories'` — **a third file**; the gate register named only the first two, and fixing two of three would leave the service list broken |

Plus the test doubles that assert the wrong URL: `apps/web/src/features/services/__tests__/tenantScope.test.tsx:100,195,219,234,309,316,335,342,390`.

**Two things that are NOT bugs and must not be "fixed":** `ServiceListPage.tsx:117`'s `to="/services/categories"` and `Sidebar.tsx:148`'s `href: '/services/categories'` are **SPA route paths**, not API calls — they stay exactly as they are. And the React-Query keys already read `tenantScopedKey(['service-categories'])` (`ServiceForm.tsx:70`, `ServiceListPage.tsx:47`, `ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:56`, `_invalidation.ts:5,23`) — **unchanged**, so CLAUDE.md rule 14's TanStack audit stays green.

> **Why this rides in this task rather than a ticket.** This task's own verification is a probe that service-category CRUD works for a `manager`. That probe cannot pass while every call goes to a route that does not exist, and the failure it would produce (404) is indistinguishable at a glance from the 403 this wave is testing for. Fixing the path is a precondition for having any evidence at all. **It is also, by itself, a user-visible repair**, so the Playwright spec below covers it.

**Files:** the seeder's two legacy methods, seven route files, the **eighty-seven** frontend call sites the census script prints across **seventeen** files plus the **six** shared-hook caller files those rows delegate to, six service-category call sites, the regenerated permission map, three self-mapped `MODULE_PERMISSIONS` rows in `usePermissions.ts`, the label bundles, and one new Playwright spec.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — declare all eighteen keys in `legacyPermissionNames()`,** grouped by module with a one-line comment naming the wave, and give `manager` the six `services.*`/`service-categories.*` keys in `legacyRolePermissionGrants()`. Twelve keys appear in **no** role block — `admin` receives them through `'admin' => self::legacyPermissionNames()` (lane `:662`) and through `ensure`'s unconditional admin grant.

- [ ] **Step 2 — gate the routes, file by file,** adding `->middleware('can:<key>')` to each route in the table above. Rules for every edit:
      - the gate goes on the **route**, never in the controller (E-2);
      - the group's existing middleware array is untouched, including `module:Workshop` / `module:Ecommerce` / `module:Inventory`;
      - `->name(...)` stays where it is;
      - one route per line-change, so the diff is readable and the reviewer can check the key against the table.

- [ ] **Step 3 — the frontend guards: every row the census script prints — all eighty-seven, across seventeen files plus the six shared-hook callers — plus the six service-category call sites and the regenerated permission map.** This is the half rev 1 did not have (plan gate r1 **B0b-4**). Work in the census table's order and do not stop at the route guards — a route guard stops a *navigation*, but every one of these pages also fires a query on mount, and a query with a stale `enabled` is a 403 the router never sees.

      **(a) The route guards.** `RequirePermission` **ANDs** `moduleKey` and `permission` (`apps/web/src/features/auth/components/RequirePermission.tsx:48-58`), so each is a one-attribute addition beside the existing module key. Post-merge lines in brackets:
      ```tsx
      // channels index [:2255], channels/new [:2265], :id/products [:2275],
      // :id/sync [:2285], :id/orders [:2295], ecommerce/orders [:2309]
      <RequirePermission moduleKey="inventory" permission="channels.view">
      // categories [:1194]
      <RequirePermission moduleKey="inventory" permission="categories.view">
      // services/:id/edit [:1627] — the D5 canonical spelling of the key
      // this wave declares. [:1591]'s services.create is already canonical.
      <RequirePermission permission="services.update">
      // services/categories [:1603] — census row 17. moduleKey="services" stays:
      // it resolves to services.view (usePermissions.ts:90), which is the RIGHT
      // check for reaching the Services area, and is NOT the right check for
      // reading the category catalogue. RequirePermission ANDs them.
      <RequirePermission moduleKey="services" permission="service-categories.view">
      ```
      > **The services subtree is asymmetric today and rev 2 only half-noticed.** At `lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:1573-1635`: `services/new` `:1591` and `services/:id/edit` `:1627` carry write permissions, while `services/categories` `:1603` — the page with category **create, edit and delete** on it — carries only `moduleKey="services"`. The index `:1579` and detail `:1615` routes keep `moduleKey="services"` alone and are correct as they are: `services.view` is exactly what they need.
      **`growth` and `growth/modules` [child routes `:3211-3218` and `:3219-3226`, inside `<Route path="growth">` at `:3210-3227`] have no wrapper at all today** — they are bare `<SuspenseWrapper>` children. Wrap each in `<RequirePermission permission="progression.view">`, matching the shape of the routes around them. **`/company-onboarding` [`:554`]** keeps `<RequireAuth>` and gains `<RequirePermission permission="companies.create">` **inside** it — the order matters, because an unauthenticated visitor must reach the login redirect, not a permission-denied surface.

      **(b) The query guards.** In each hook or page, take `const { hasPermission } = usePermissions()` and add the flag to the existing `enabled`:
      ```tsx
      // ChannelListPage.tsx:24
      enabled: Boolean(tenantId && companyId) && hasPermission('channels.view'),
      // ChannelCreateWizard.tsx:13-16 — it has NO enabled option today, so add one
      enabled: hasPermission('channels.view'),
      // useAggregateChannelOrders.ts:22
      enabled: tenantId !== null && companyId !== null && hasPermission('channels.view'),
      // useCategories.ts:114 (the tree query CategoriesPage.tsx:27 consumes)
      enabled: !!tenantId && !!companyId && hasPermission('categories.view'),
      // useCompanyProgression.ts:89 and :104, useModuleReadiness.ts:25,
      // useRecommendations.ts:24
      enabled: !!tenantId && !!companyId && hasPermission('progression.view'),
      // ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:61 — census row 18 / A35
      // ServiceListPage.tsx:52          — census row 21 / A45
      // ServiceForm.tsx:75              — census row 22 / A41
      enabled: !!tenantId && !!companyId && hasPermission('service-categories.view'),
      // ServicePicker.tsx:103 — A1, NEW in rev 5 (plan gate r4 B4-1). It is a
      // shared molecule, not a page, so no route guard can reach it: it is
      // mounted from DocumentLineEditor.tsx:1261-1287 behind only the Workshop
      // module and from BundleComponentFormModal.tsx:335-343 behind no service
      // check at all. Keep the existing four conditions and AND the key.
      enabled: searchEnabled && !disabled && tenantId !== null && companyId !== null
        && hasPermission('services.view'),
      // ServiceDetailPage.tsx:57 — A39. The route is guarded moduleKey="services"
      // (-> services.view), so this is belt-and-braces on the same key.
      // ServiceForm.tsx:87 — A42. The edit route is guarded services.UPDATE, which
      // does NOT imply services.view, so this one is load-bearing: an actor with
      // update-but-not-view would otherwise fire a GET the server refuses.
      enabled: Boolean(id) && !!tenantId && !!companyId && hasPermission('services.view'),
      ```

      > **What `ServicePicker` shows when the key is absent, stated so the executing agent does not invent it.** The component keeps rendering — it is a search input inside someone else's form — and the query simply never fires, so the listbox stays empty and `isLoading` stays `false`. **Both live callers must degrade legibly rather than silently:** in `DocumentLineEditor.tsx:1261-1287` the service line-type option and its picker render only when `hasPermission('services.view')` is true, so a document editor without the key sees the product path and no dead search box; in `BundleComponentFormModal.tsx:335-343` (the workshop-bundle labor form) the labor component type is likewise offered only with the key. **Do not** disable the picker with a tooltip and **do not** show an empty-result message — a control that exists only to refuse is the same defect one layer down. Record both caller edits in the handback with their line numbers.
      **(b2) The `/categories` transitive callers — SIX of them, and rev 4 had two (plan gate r4 B4-1, extended).** `categories.view` is admin-only, and the census shows six live production callers of a `/categories` read, not two. Each one passes the flag into its hook; **no hook is edited to gate itself**, for exactly the reason ruling **(B)** gives — a hook-level gate hides the decision from the surface that owns it and would catch consumers this wave never inspected.

      `features/catalog/api/queries.ts`'s three query hooks (`useCategories` `:59`, `useCategoryTree` `:76`, `useCategory` `:92`) **already** accept an `options` object and already AND `options?.enabled ?? true` into their `enabled` (`:69`, `:85`, `:105`), so those three callers need **no hook change at all**:

Ruling **(B)** already adds the optional, defaulted argument to `useCategoryTree`; **`useCategories` needs the same treatment**, in the same shape, because `CategorySelector` consumes it:

That is the correct behaviour **given** an admin-only `categories.view`; whether the key should be admin-only when a category *picker* sits on four operator forms is a **product** question, filed as residual **R-0b-10** for owner ruling before the wave deploys. **Do not resolve it by widening the key in this task** — the key's template is settled by spec §8 — and **do not** resolve it by leaving the callers unguarded, which is the defect B0b-4 exists to close.

      > **`useCategoryTree()` is gated at its two callers, NOT inside the hook — RULED above (B).** The `useCategories.ts:114` line in the list above is therefore **not** edited in place: the hook gains `options: { enabled?: boolean } = {}` and `CategoriesPage.tsx:27` and `CategoryForm.tsx:26` each pass `{ enabled: hasPermission('categories.view') }`. Rev 2 left the choice to handback time; it is made here, with the caller enumeration that forced it.

      | Service categories **+ services** | `service-categories.view` / `.create` / `.update`, and `services.view` / `.create` / `.update` | `apps/web/src/features/services/__tests__/serviceCategories.permissions.test.tsx` *(new)*. **Rev 5 widens this file's subject**: it also owns both "Add Service" link assertions (census rows B19–B20) and the **PATCH-not-PUT** assertion (row A44, plan gate r4 **M4-1**), because all three live on the same two components and the same mock boundary |
      | Services detail + picker | `services.view` / `.update` | `apps/web/src/features/services/__tests__/serviceDetail.permissions.test.tsx` *(new)* — `ServiceDetailPage`'s edit link and delete button (rows B13, A40) — and `apps/web/src/components/molecules/pickers/__tests__/ServicePicker.permissions.test.tsx` *(new)*, the shared molecule no route guard can reach (row A1). Both follow `features/services/__tests__/tenantScope.test.tsx`'s mock block |
      | Category consumers | `categories.view` | `apps/web/src/features/categories/components/__tests__/CategorySelector.permissions.test.tsx` *(new)* — one representative of the six transitive `/categories` callers (row A11). Assert the zero-versus-one on `fetchCategories`; the other five are the same edit and are covered by the census re-run, not by five near-identical specs |

      **Before creating any of them, run `ls` on the directory** — three of the five feature folders already have a `__tests__` directory with sibling specs, and a new file must follow the neighbours' harness (`renderWithProviders`, the `vi.mock` of the auth store) rather than invent one. If a matching spec already exists for that surface, **add the pair to it** instead of creating a second file; say which you did, per feature, in the handback. The `AddCompanyModal` pair is the one that cannot follow a live caller — assert on the component directly, as its two existing specs already do (`__tests__/AddCompanyModal.ordering.test.tsx:48`, `features/settings/__tests__/TunisiaLocalization.test.tsx:81`): with `companies.create` the form renders and the submit button is enabled; without it the component renders `null` and no `POST /companies` is issued.

      **The bodies, supplied and REWRITTEN AGAINST THE REAL HARNESS — plan gate r4 MAJOR M4-2.** Rev 4 called its five bodies executable and simultaneously told the executor to adapt the imports, the harness names and the settling assertions. They were not executable: every one of them imported `@/test/harness`, **which does not exist on `dev` or on the lane**; the progression body imported a `CompanyProfilePage` and a `./fixtures` that do not exist; the CompanySelector case rendered a component it never imported, inside another file's fenced block; and four cases awaited `getByRole('main')`, which the plan itself labelled a placeholder. Rev 5 supplies six complete files, each mirroring the **neighbouring spec in its own directory**, verified against `lane/w-lot-a-1a` `52f5ad796`.

**The harness, once, so no file has to guess.** There are exactly two shared helpers and both are real:

| Helper | Path | What it gives |
|---|---|---|
| `renderWithProviders(ui, opts)` | `apps/web/src/test/renderWithProviders.tsx:82` | `QueryClientProvider` + `MemoryRouter` + `ProductConfigProvider` + `CompanyConfigProvider`, and it **returns the `queryClient`** alongside RTL's result (`:113`). `createTestQueryClient()` at `:52` sets `retry: false, gcTime: 0`. |
| `seedAuth({ permissions })` / `resetAuth()` | `apps/web/src/test/seedAuth.ts:26` / `:63` | seeds `useAuthStore` (including an optional `permissions` array, `:32,:44`) and `useCompanyStore.currentCompanyId`. **This is the lever**: `hasPermission()` reads `user.permissions` first (`usePermissions.ts:203-206`), so the two arms differ by that one field and never by a role name. |

**The settle assertion is the query's own `fetchStatus`, not a landmark.** Every negative arm asserts that the query **registered in the cache and never started**:
```ts
await waitFor(() => {
  const q = queryClient.getQueryCache().getAll()
    .find((e) => Array.isArray(e.queryKey) && e.queryKey[0] === '<namespace>')
  expect(q).toBeDefined()               // the component mounted and the hook ran
  expect(q?.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')   // `enabled` was false
})
expect(mockFetch).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
```
That is three facts, not one: the tree rendered, the hook ran, and `enabled` refused. It cannot pass on an unmounted tree the way `expect(x).toHaveLength(0)` can, and it needs no `getByRole('main')` — **which no page in this set renders**. The positive arm is `await waitFor(() => expect(mockFetch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1))`.

**Six files, not five** — CompanySelector is staged separately, so it is written separately (plan gate r4 **minor 2**).
