// 2. apps/web/src/features/categories/__tests__/CategoriesPage.permissions.test.tsx
// FOUR keys: the tree query on categories.view, and the three controls on
// .create / .update / .delete. CategoryTreeView renders each row control purely
// on PROP PRESENCE (CategoryTreeView.tsx:84,93,102), so "absent" means the
// button is not in the DOM at all.
// Boundary: features/categories/api/categoriesApi.ts, which the hooks call
// (useCategories.ts:99,113).
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest'
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'

import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/renderWithProviders'
import { seedAuth, resetAuth } from '@/test/seedAuth'

import { CategoriesPage } from '../CategoriesPage'

const mockFetchCategoryTree = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())

vi.mock('../api/categoriesApi', () => ({
  fetchCategoryTree: mockFetchCategoryTree,
  fetchCategories: vi.fn(),
  fetchCategory: vi.fn(),
  createCategory: vi.fn(),
  updateCategory: vi.fn(),
  deleteCategory: vi.fn(),
  reorderCategories: vi.fn(),
}))

vi.mock('react-i18next', () => ({
  useTranslation: () => ({ t: (key: string, fallback?: string) => fallback ?? key }),
}))

vi.mock('sonner', () => ({ toast: { success: vi.fn(), error: vi.fn() } }))

const CATEGORY_ROW = {
  id: 1,
  name: 'Drinks',
  parent_id: null,
  children: [],
  is_active: true,
}

beforeEach(() => {
  mockFetchCategoryTree.mockReset()
  mockFetchCategoryTree.mockResolvedValue([CATEGORY_ROW])
})

afterEach(() => {
  resetAuth()
})

describe('CategoriesPage — categories.* (census rows A12, A14-A17)', () => {
  it('registers the tree query but never fetches, and shows no create control, without categories.view', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: [] })

    const { queryClient } = renderWithProviders(<CategoriesPage />)

    await waitFor(() => {
      const q = queryClient.getQueryCache().getAll()
        .find((e) => Array.isArray(e.queryKey) && e.queryKey[0] === 'categories' && e.queryKey[1] === 'tree')
      expect(q).toBeDefined()
      expect(q?.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
    })
    expect(mockFetchCategoryTree).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('fetches the tree exactly once with categories.view', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['categories.view'] })

    renderWithProviders(<CategoriesPage />)

    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockFetchCategoryTree).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1) })
  })

  it('renders no create control with view alone and renders it with categories.create', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['categories.view'] })
    const first = renderWithProviders(<CategoriesPage />)
    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockFetchCategoryTree).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1) })
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /categories\.actions\.create/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    first.unmount()

    mockFetchCategoryTree.mockClear()
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['categories.view', 'categories.create'] })
    renderWithProviders(<CategoriesPage />)
    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockFetchCategoryTree).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1) })
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /categories\.actions\.create/i }).length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })

  it('passes onEdit/onDelete to the tree only for categories.update/.delete', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['categories.view'] })
    const first = renderWithProviders(<CategoriesPage />)
    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockFetchCategoryTree).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1) })
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /categories\.actions\.edit/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /categories\.actions\.delete/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    first.unmount()

    mockFetchCategoryTree.mockClear()
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['categories.view', 'categories.update', 'categories.delete'] })
    renderWithProviders(<CategoriesPage />)
    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockFetchCategoryTree).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1) })
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /categories\.actions\.edit/i }).length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: /categories\.actions\.delete/i }).length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })
})
~~~

> **The control `name:` regexes above assume the `t()` mock returns the KEY.** Read the three `t(...)` calls at `CategoriesPage.tsx:140-146,:172-178` and `CategoryTreeView.tsx:84,93,102` at the merge moment and use the **actual** keys; if a control renders an icon with no accessible text, add the `aria-label` its neighbour already uses rather than asserting on a `className`. **Do not** relax the assertion to `queryAllByRole('button')` — a count is not an identity.

// 3. apps/web/src/features/progression/__tests__/progression.permissions.test.tsx
// The page is GrowthPage (features/progression/pages/GrowthPage.tsx:3), which
// renders GrowthDashboard; there is NO CompanyProfilePage (plan gate r4 M4-2).
// The four queries go through progressionApi, mocked exactly as the neighbour

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest'
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'

import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/renderWithProviders'

const mockGetProfile = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())
const mockGetMilestones = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())
const mockGetModules = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())
const mockGetRecommendations = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())

vi.mock('../api/progressionApi', () => ({
  progressionApi: {
    getProfile: mockGetProfile,
    register: vi.fn(),
    getMilestones: mockGetMilestones,
    getModules: mockGetModules,
    activateModule: vi.fn(),
    getRecommendations: mockGetRecommendations,
    acceptRecommendation: vi.fn(),
    dismissRecommendation: vi.fn(),
  },
}))

vi.mock('sonner', () => ({ toast: { success: vi.fn(), error: vi.fn() } }))
vi.mock('i18next', () => ({ default: { t: (key: string) => key } }))
vi.mock('react-i18next', () => ({
  useTranslation: () => ({ t: (key: string, fallback?: string) => fallback ?? key }),
}))

// Inline literals, not a nonexistent ./fixtures module (plan gate r4 M4-2).
// Shape read off the two components' props at the merge moment.
const MODULE_FIXTURE = {
  module_id: 'mod-1',
  name: 'Loyalty',
  status: 'available',
  readiness_score: 80,
  blockers: [],
}

const RECOMMENDATION_FIXTURE = {
  id: 'rec-1',
  title: 'Enable loyalty',
  body: 'Reward repeat customers.',
  status: 'pending',
}

beforeEach(() => {
  mockGetProfile.mockReset(); mockGetProfile.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'p-1', current_stage: 'launch' })
  mockGetMilestones.mockReset(); mockGetMilestones.mockResolvedValue([])
  mockGetModules.mockReset(); mockGetModules.mockResolvedValue([])
  mockGetRecommendations.mockReset(); mockGetRecommendations.mockResolvedValue([])
})

afterEach(() => {
  resetAuth()
})

const progressionCalls = (): number =>
  mockGetProfile.mock.calls.length + mockGetMilestones.mock.calls.length
  + mockGetModules.mock.calls.length + mockGetRecommendations.mock.calls.length

describe('GrowthPage — progression.view (census rows A27, A29-A30, A32)', () => {

    seedAuth({ permissions: [] })

    const { queryClient } = renderWithProviders(<GrowthPage />)

    await waitFor(() => {
      const registered = queryClient.getQueryCache().getAll()
        .filter((e) => Array.isArray(e.queryKey) && e.queryKey[0] === 'progression')
      expect(registered.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
      expect(registered.every((e) => e.state.fetchStatus === 'idle')).toBe(true)
    })
    expect(progressionCalls()).toBe(0)
  })

  it('fires every progression query exactly once with progression.view', async () => {

    renderWithProviders(<GrowthPage />)

    // Four call sites: useCompanyProgression's two (:89, :104), useModuleReadiness
    // (:25) and useRecommendations (:24). If GrowthDashboard does not mount all
    // four, assert the ones it does and say which in the handback — do NOT
    // change the number to whatever the run printed.
    await waitFor(() => { expect(progressionCalls()).toBe(4) })
  })
})

describe('Progression action controls — progression.manage (census rows A31, A33-A34)', () => {
  it('hides the module Activate control without progression.manage', () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['progression.view'] })
    renderWithProviders(<ModuleCard module={MODULE_FIXTURE} />)
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /activate/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('shows the module Activate control with progression.manage', () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['progression.view', 'progression.manage'] })
    renderWithProviders(<ModuleCard module={MODULE_FIXTURE} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /activate/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('hides Accept and Dismiss without progression.manage and shows both with it', () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['progression.view'] })
    const first = renderWithProviders(<RecommendationCard recommendation={RECOMMENDATION_FIXTURE} />)
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /accept/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /dismiss/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    first.unmount()

    seedAuth({ permissions: ['progression.view', 'progression.manage'] })
    renderWithProviders(<RecommendationCard recommendation={RECOMMENDATION_FIXTURE} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /accept/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /dismiss/i })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})

// 6. apps/web/src/features/services/__tests__/serviceCategories.permissions.test.tsx
// Neighbour harness: features/services/__tests__/tenantScope.test.tsx — SAME
// DIRECTORY. It mocks '../../../lib/api' with get/post/put/delete (:24-31),
// stubs react-router-dom's useParams/useNavigate/Link (:33-40), stubs
// useCompany, useTaxConfigName and TaxConfigurationField (:48-59). This file
// copies that block verbatim and adds `patch`, which the ServiceForm change in
// Step 3(d) now needs (plan gate r4 M4-1).
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest'
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/renderWithProviders'
import { seedAuth, resetAuth } from '@/test/seedAuth'

import { ServiceCategoryListPage } from '../ServiceCategoryListPage'
import { ServiceListPage } from '../ServiceListPage'
import { ServiceForm } from '../ServiceForm'

const mockApiGet = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())
const mockApiPost = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())
const mockApiPatch = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())
const mockApiPut = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())
const mockApiDelete = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn())

vi.mock('../../../lib/api', () => ({
  api: {
    get: mockApiGet,
    post: mockApiPost,
    patch: mockApiPatch,
    put: mockApiPut,
    delete: mockApiDelete,
  },
}))

vi.mock('react-router-dom', async () => {
  const actual = await vi.importActual<typeof import('react-router-dom')>('react-router-dom')
  return {
    ...actual,
    useParams: () => ({ id: 's-123' }),
    useNavigate: () => vi.fn(),
    Link: ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => children,
  }
})

vi.mock('react-i18next', () => ({
  useTranslation: () => ({ t: (k: string, fallback?: string) => fallback ?? k }),
}))

vi.mock('../../../hooks/useCompany', () => ({
  useCompany: () => ({ currentCompany: { currency: 'TND' } }),
}))
vi.mock('../../../hooks/useTaxConfigName', () => ({ useTaxConfigName: () => null }))
vi.mock('../../../components/molecules/TaxConfigurationField', () => ({
  TaxConfigurationField: () => null,
}))

const categoryCalls = (): unknown[] =>
  mockApiGet.mock.calls.filter(([url]) => String(url).startsWith('/service-categories'))

beforeEach(() => {
  mockApiGet.mockReset()
  mockApiGet.mockImplementation(async (url: string) => {
    if (String(url).startsWith('/service-categories')) { return { data: { data: [] } } }
    if (String(url).startsWith('/services/')) {
      return { data: { data: { id: 's-123', code: 'SVC-1', name: 'Service', description: null,
        pricing_type: 'flat_rate', base_price: '0', currency: 'TND', is_active: true,
        tax_rate: null, default_tax_configuration_id: null, category_id: null, category: null,
        default_duration_minutes: null, hourly_rate: null, created_at: null, updated_at: null } } }
    }
    return { data: { data: [], meta: { total: 0 } } }
  })
  mockApiPost.mockReset(); mockApiPost.mockResolvedValue({ data: { data: { id: 's-new' } } })
  mockApiPatch.mockReset(); mockApiPatch.mockResolvedValue({ data: { data: { id: 's-123' } } })
  mockApiPut.mockReset()
  mockApiDelete.mockReset(); mockApiDelete.mockResolvedValue({ data: undefined })
})

afterEach(() => {
  resetAuth()
})

describe('service-categories.* — three callers, one key (census rows A35, A41, A45)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['ServiceCategoryListPage', () => <ServiceCategoryListPage />],
    ['ServiceListPage', () => <ServiceListPage />],
    ['ServiceForm', () => <ServiceForm />],
  ])('%s issues NO /service-categories request without the key', async (_name, element) => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['services.view'] })

    const { queryClient } = renderWithProviders(element())

    await waitFor(() => {
      const q = queryClient.getQueryCache().getAll()
        .find((e) => Array.isArray(e.queryKey) && e.queryKey[0] === 'service-categories')
      expect(q).toBeDefined()
      expect(q?.state.fetchStatus).toBe('idle')
    })
    expect(categoryCalls()).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it.each([
    ['ServiceCategoryListPage', () => <ServiceCategoryListPage />],
    ['ServiceListPage', () => <ServiceListPage />],
    ['ServiceForm', () => <ServiceForm />],
  ])('%s issues exactly one /service-categories request with the key', async (_name, element) => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['services.view', 'service-categories.view'] })

    renderWithProviders(element())

    await waitFor(() => { expect(categoryCalls()).toHaveLength(1) })
  })

  it('hides the "Manage Categories" link without service-categories.view (census row B18)', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['services.view'] })

    renderWithProviders(<ServiceListPage />)

    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockApiGet).toHaveBeenCalled() })
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Manage Categories/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('hides Add Category / Edit / Delete without the create and update keys (rows A36-A38)', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['services.view', 'service-categories.view'] })

    renderWithProviders(<ServiceCategoryListPage />)

    await waitFor(() => { expect(categoryCalls()).toHaveLength(1) })
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Add Category/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /edit/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /delete/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('hides BOTH "Add Service" links without services.create (census rows B19-B20)', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['services.view'] })

    renderWithProviders(<ServiceListPage />)

    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockApiGet).toHaveBeenCalled() })
    expect(screen.queryAllByText(/Add Service/i)).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('sends PATCH, never PUT, when a service is updated (plan gate r4 M4-1, census row A44)', async () => {
    seedAuth({ permissions: ['services.view', 'services.update', 'service-categories.view'] })

    renderWithProviders(<ServiceForm />)

    await waitFor(() => { expect(mockApiGet).toHaveBeenCalled() })
    // Submit the loaded form. The locator is the form's own submit control —
    // read it at ServiceForm.tsx's footer at the merge moment.
    screen.getByRole('button', { name: /save|submit/i }).click()

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockApiPatch).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/services/s-123', expect.anything())
    })
    expect(mockApiPut).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })
})
~~~

> **The three `Link`-text assertions use `queryByText`, not `queryByRole('link')`, on purpose.** The neighbour spec stubs `react-router-dom`'s `Link` to render its children only (`tenantScope.test.tsx:38`), so there is no anchor element and no `link` role in this harness. Rev 4's `queryByRole('link', …)` would have passed vacuously — green whether or not the guard existed. If you drop the `Link` stub, switch to the role query; **do not keep both**.

> **Every one of the six files above must be RUN and seen RED before its guard is written**, and the red must name the guard's absence — a fetch that happened, a control that was found. A body that is green on its first run is not testing the guard; find out why before you write a line of `ServicePicker.tsx`.

      > **`it.each` in file 5 is deliberate.** Three callers, one key, one expectation — writing it three times invites fixing two of the three, which is the exact defect rev 2's census had (`ServiceListPage` was the third file the gate register never named).

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature\Identity;

use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\LegacyRoleBaseline;
use Database\Seeders\RolesAndPermissionsSeeder;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test;
use Tests\TestCase;

/**
 * The constant and the CATALOGUE agree. Ships with Task 6, the commit that
 * declares the last of the nineteen keys — an undeclared key is a silent 403 for
 * every caller including admin, because Spatie 6.25 swallows
 * PermissionDoesNotExist in checkPermissionTo.
 */
final class Wave0bAdditionCatalogueTest extends TestCase
{
    #[Test]
    public function every_wave_0b_addition_is_declared_in_the_seeder(): void
    {
        $declared = RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::permissionNames();

        foreach (LegacyRoleBaseline::wave0bAdditions('admin') as $key) {
            $this->assertContains(
                $key,
                $declared,
                "key '{$key}' is in WAVE_0B_ADDITIONS and gates a live route, but is not in the catalogue — "
                .'Spatie fails closed and every caller including admin gets 403.',
            );
        }
    }

    #[Test]
    public function the_catalogue_grew_by_exactly_nineteen(): void
    {
        $this->assertCount(19, LegacyRoleBaseline::wave0bAdditions('admin'));
    }

    #[Test]
    public function the_seven_grant_to_keys_reach_manager_and_general_manager_in_the_seeder(): void
    {
        $grants = RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::rolePermissionGrants();

        foreach (['manager', 'general_manager'] as $role) {
            foreach (LegacyRoleBaseline::wave0bAdditions('manager') as $key) {
                $this->assertContains($key, $grants[$role], "the seeder does not give {$role} '{$key}', but the deploy's --grant-to does. A NEW tenant would differ from a migrated one.");
            }
        }
    }
}

## Task 7 (0b-5): gate the two BatchExpiry writes **beside** `BatchActionAccess`

**The lane already attaches middleware to both routes** — `BatchActionAccess::class.':batches.delete'` at lane `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php:27` and `:batches.recall` at `:30`. **That is not coverage.** The middleware returns `$next($request)` with **no permission check at all** while `LotActionPermissionActivation::enforced()` is false (`lane/…/Presentation/Middleware/BatchActionAccess.php:18-20`), and `false` is the shipped default (`lane/…/config/lot_action_permissions.php:5`). Counting it as a gate would mark two live writes as gated on every tenant that has not flipped the flag.

**0b-5 adds a plain `can:` beside it, never instead of it.** The route then gates unconditionally, and the lane's flag keeps governing only the lane's own stricter behaviour.

**Files:** `apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php` (lane `:27,30`), the ratchet test, the baseline.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — both keys already exist.** `batches.delete` and `batches.recall` are in the seeded catalogue; the audit called them "dead" **precisely because** these two routes had no gate. `batches.recall` is additionally re-granted by the lane to `general_manager` and revoked from `manager`. **Neither key is declared or moved by this wave**, and neither may be deprecated in wave 1 — §4.9.3's four exclusions.

- [ ] **Step 2 — stack the gates:**
      ```php
      Route::delete('/batches/{uuid}', [BatchController::class, 'destroy'])
          ->middleware([BatchActionAccess::class.':batches.delete', 'can:batches.delete']);

      Route::post('/batches/{uuid}/recall', [BatchController::class, 'recall'])
          ->middleware([BatchActionAccess::class.':batches.recall', 'can:batches.recall']);
      ```
      Stacked middleware is an **AND** by construction; nothing new is needed.

- [ ] **Step 3 — the classifier must still refuse the flag-conditional middleware.** `RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest::a_flag_conditional_middleware_is_not_gating` (wave 0a) pins `BatchActionAccess` as the worked example. It must **stay green after this change** — the route is now gated by the `can:` half, and the assertion is about the *classifier's alias list*, not about the route. If it goes red, the classifier has started accepting `BatchActionAccess`, which is the defect it exists to prevent.

- [ ] **Step 4 — delete the two baseline entries, lower `UNCOVERED_WRITE_CEILING` by 2, run the batch suite, commit.**
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b/apps/api && ./vendor/bin/phpunit tests/Architecture/RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest.php tests/Feature/BatchExpiry
      ```
      ```
      git add apps/api/app/Modules/BatchExpiry/Presentation/routes.php apps/api/tests/Architecture/RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest.php apps/api/tests/Architecture/baselines/route-permission-coverage-baseline.json
      ```
      ```
      Phase 0.2.7: Gate the batch delete and recall writes BESIDE BatchActionAccess

      lane/w-lot-a-1a attaches BatchActionAccess to both routes, but that middleware
      returns $next() with no check at all while LOT_ACTION_PERMISSIONS_ENFORCE is
      false — its shipped default — so it is not coverage, and the ratchet's
      classifier already pins it as the worked example of a flag-conditional
      middleware that is not a gate. A plain can:batches.delete / can:batches.recall
      is stacked BESIDE it (stacked middleware is an AND), so the routes are gated
      unconditionally while the lane's flag keeps governing only the lane's own
      stricter behaviour.

      Neither key is declared or moved here — both are already seeded, and both are
      among the four keys §4.9.3 excludes from deprecation for exactly this reason.

      Location scope on these two writes is a SEPARATE, filed gap: neither consults
      LocationScopeResolver. Ticket
      docs/superpowers/tickets/2026-09-10-batch-writes-ignore-location-scope.md,
      wave 3.

      Spec 4.4.3, 4.4.4, 8 (wave 0b, 0b-5).

      Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>

## Task 9 (0b-12): `RequireAnyPermission` — the failing permission list in `error.details` — **edited on top of the merged T2 state**

`lane/t2-receipt-spine` already replaces the hard-coded *"You do not have permission to view transaction destinations."* (`dev:apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php:25`) — a copy-paste from its first caller that is wrong on every other route — with a generic message. **0b-12 is the half T2 does not do:** naming which permissions would have satisfied the gate.

> **The message is already known, so half this task is verification, not authorship** (plan gate r2 **B2-1**). T2's string is, byte for byte:
> ```php
> 'message' => 'You do not have permission to perform this action.',
> ```
> at `lane/t2-receipt-spine:apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php:25`. Rev 2's *"read the message it settled on"* left that to execution time; it is pinned here instead, and Step 1 becomes an assertion. The two refs differ in **exactly that one line** — `git diff dev...lane/t2-receipt-spine -- apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php` is a single-line hunk — so the `code`, the `meta` block and the 403 status are `dev`'s and stay `dev`'s. **What T2 does NOT ship is `error.details`.** That half is the real work, and it is why this task exists.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — assert T2's message survived the merge, byte for byte. Verification only; author nothing here.**
      ```
      grep -n "You do not have permission to perform this action." apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php
      ```
      **Expected:** one hit, at the `'message' =>` line inside the `if (! $allowed)` block. **If instead you find `"…to view transaction destinations."`, T2 has not merged and the wave should not have started** (Phase 0). **Do not re-word the message** — that would be two branches rewriting one string, which is precisely why 0a-9 moved here.
- [ ] **Step 2 — add `details`** to the 403 body, beside the existing `code` and `message`. This is the **whole** production diff of this task — one added key, on the block at `lane/t2-receipt-spine:apps/api/app/Http/Middleware/RequireAnyPermission.php:22-31`:
      ```php
      if (! $allowed) {
          return response()->json([
              'error' => [
                  'code' => 'FORBIDDEN',
                  // T2's generic message, byte-identical, NOT re-worded.
                  'message' => 'You do not have permission to perform this action.',
                  // 0b-12: name WHICH permissions would have satisfied the gate.
                  // $permissions is the variadic route argument; array_values()
                  // because a variadic is already a list and the client contract
                  // is a JSON array, never an object with integer keys.
                  'details' => ['required_any_of' => array_values($permissions)],
              ],
              'meta' => [
                  'timestamp' => now()->toIso8601String(),
                  'request_id' => $request->header('X-Request-ID', (string) uuid_create()),
              ],
          ], 403);
      }
      ```
      Nothing else in the file changes: not the signature `handle(Request $request, Closure $next, string ...$permissions): Response` at `:14`, not the `$allowed` expression at `:17-19`, not the `return $next($request)` at `:34`.
- [ ] **Step 3 — the test, written out** (plan gate r2 **M2-5**; rev 2 described it and supplied no body). Create **`apps/api/tests/Feature/Http/RequireAnyPermissionDetailsTest.php`** — that directory exists and already holds the repository's other middleware-envelope tests (`dev:apps/api/tests/Feature/Http/ApiErrorEnvelopeTest.php`, `TrustedProxyTest.php`), and **neither `dev` nor T2 has any existing `RequireAnyPermission` test to sit beside** (`git grep -l 'RequireAnyPermission' <ref> -- apps/api/tests` is empty on both). The probe route is registered in `setUp()`, exactly as `ApiErrorEnvelopeTest.php:28-32` does, so the assertion does not depend on the fixtures of the one production caller (`dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Company/routes.php:99`) — but the production caller's own key list is asserted too, as the pin that the middleware is really wired.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature\Http;

use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\User;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test;
use Tests\TestCase;

/**
 * 0b-12. RequireAnyPermission's 403 body must NAME the permissions that would
 * have satisfied the gate, in error.details.required_any_of — and must change
 * nothing else about the envelope.
 *
 * A 403 body is a client contract. lane/t2-receipt-spine already replaced the
 * hard-coded "…to view transaction destinations." with a generic message; this
 * wave adds `details` BESIDE it and re-words nothing, so the message assertion
 * below is deliberately a byte-for-byte literal rather than a partial match.
 */
final class RequireAnyPermissionDetailsTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;

    /** The exact string lane/t2-receipt-spine settled on, at its :25. */
    private const T2_MESSAGE = 'You do not have permission to perform this action.';

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();

        $this->app['router']
            ->middleware(['api', 'require.any.permission:alpha.one,beta.two'])
            ->get('/api/v1/__require-any-permission-probe', static fn (): array => ['data' => ['ok' => true]]);
    }

    #[Test]
    public function the_403_names_every_permission_that_would_have_satisfied_the_gate(): void
    {
        // A user holding neither key. Spatie 6.25 swallows PermissionDoesNotExist
        // in checkPermissionTo, so an undeclared key is simply `false` here — which
        // is the same code path a real holder-less caller takes.
        $response = $this->actingAs(User::factory()->create())
            ->getJson('/api/v1/__require-any-permission-probe');

        $response->assertStatus(403);
        $response->assertJsonPath('error.details.required_any_of', ['alpha.one', 'beta.two']);
    }

    #[Test]
    public function the_rest_of_the_envelope_is_byte_identical_to_the_one_t2_settled_on(): void
    {
        $response = $this->actingAs(User::factory()->create())
            ->getJson('/api/v1/__require-any-permission-probe');

        $response->assertStatus(403);
        $response->assertJsonPath('error.code', 'FORBIDDEN');
        $response->assertJsonPath('error.message', self::T2_MESSAGE);

        /** @var array{error: array<string, mixed>, meta: array<string, mixed>} $body */
        $body = $response->json();

        // Exactly three keys under `error`, and exactly the two `meta` keys the
        // middleware has always emitted. A fourth key, or a renamed one, is a
        // client-contract change and must fail here rather than in a browser.
        self::assertSame(['code', 'message', 'details'], array_keys($body['error']));
        self::assertSame(['timestamp', 'request_id'], array_keys($body['meta']));
        self::assertSame(['required_any_of'], array_keys($body['error']['details']));
    }

    #[Test]
    public function a_holder_of_any_one_of_the_named_permissions_passes_through(): void
    {
        $user = User::factory()->create();
        $user->givePermissionTo(
            \Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission::create(['name' => 'beta.two', 'guard_name' => 'sanctum']),
        );

        $this->actingAs($user)
            ->getJson('/api/v1/__require-any-permission-probe')
            ->assertOk()
            ->assertJsonPath('data.ok', true);
    }

    /**
     * The pin that the middleware is actually wired to something: the ONE
     * production caller in the tree keeps its five-key list. If a route file
     * drops the middleware, the two probe cases above would still pass.
     */
    #[Test]
    public function the_one_production_caller_still_declares_its_five_keys(): void
    {
        $route = collect($this->app['router']->getRoutes())->first(
            static fn ($r): bool => $r->getName() === 'company.locations.transaction-destinations',
        );

        self::assertNotNull($route, 'the transaction-destinations route no longer exists');
        self::assertContains(
            'require.any.permission:inventory.transfers.create,inventory.transfer,purchase-orders.receive,repositories.manage,document-ingestions.create',
            $route->gatherMiddleware(),
        );
    }
}
~~~

      **Red first, and it is one line:** run the file **before** Step 2's edit. `the_403_names_every_permission_that_would_have_satisfied_the_gate` fails on `Property [error.details.required_any_of] does not exist`, and `the_rest_of_the_envelope…` fails on `assertSame(['code','message','details'], ['code','message'])`. Capture both verbatim. The other two cases are green before and after — they are the *unchanged*-envelope half, and a case that is green throughout is the correct shape for "this must not move".

## Task 10 (0b-6): delete `PermissionSeeder.php`, its caller, two tests, and re-point five

**Definition of done, stated so it is not read as a one-line deletion:** `PermissionSeeder.php` gone, `ProductionSeeder.php:73-76` gone, **two tests deleted**, **five tests re-pointed and green**.

The file ships a colliding taxonomy — 65 different permission strings and seven **capitalised** role names (`Administrator`, `Sales Manager`, `Accountant`, `Sales Rep`, `Warehouse Manager`, `Receptionist`, `Cashier`) that would collide with the real lowercase `accountant`/`cashier` if it ever ran. Its only production caller is manual (`ProductionSeeder`, never in the entrypoint or the provisioning path).

| Test file | Reference | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/GuardConsistencyTest.php:17,113-129` | imports it; asserts *"PermissionSeeder created permissions/roles with the correct guard"* | **DELETED with its subject.** The guard invariant it asserts is re-asserted for the **real** catalogue by `EnsurePermissionsCustomisationGuardTest` (every created row is `guard_name = 'sanctum'`) and, in wave 1, by `PermissionsSyncTest`. Expect a `-N lines` diff, not a regression. |
| `apps/api/tests/Feature/Pricing/PricingPermissionSeederTest.php:15,34,73,117-126` | the class **is** its subject; one case calls it *"currently inert"* | **DELETED with it.** |
| `apps/api/tests/Feature/Pricing/CheckMarginTest.php:19,66` | `$this->seed(PermissionSeeder::class)` in `setUp` to obtain pricing permissions | **RE-POINTED** at `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`. |
| `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/DocumentAdditionalCostTest.php:25,70` | same shape | **RE-POINTED** |
| `apps/api/tests/Feature/Taxation/TaxConfigurationManagementTest.php:17,37` | same | **RE-POINTED** |
| `apps/api/tests/Feature/Taxation/TaxBreakdownEndpointTest.php:21,49` | same | **RE-POINTED** |
| `apps/api/tests/Feature/Taxation/TaxConfigurationCapabilityDelegationTest.php:16,72` | same | **RE-POINTED** |

> **The five re-points are the real work.** Each currently receives `PermissionSeeder`'s **capitalised** role names; re-pointing them at the real seeder gives them the lowercase seeded roles, so **every assertion that references a role by name must be read and adjusted in the same commit**. Do not assume `str_replace` is enough.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — delete the caller first,** so nothing can seed the class while you work: `apps/api/database/seeders/ProductionSeeder.php:73-76` (the `[6/6]` block, both `info()` lines and the `$this->call(PermissionSeeder::class)`). Renumber the surviving step labels (`[5/6]` → `[5/5]`) so the console output is not a lie.
- [ ] **Step 2 — delete the two tests, re-point the five, run each by path**, fixing role-name assertions as you go.
- [ ] **Step 3 — delete `apps/api/database/seeders/PermissionSeeder.php`.**
- [ ] **Step 4 — prove nothing else references it:**
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b && grep -rn "PermissionSeeder" apps/api --include='*.php' --include='*.json' --include='*.sh' ; echo "exit=$?"
      ```
      **Expected: no output** (grep exit 1). Quote the `--include` globs — an unquoted glob is a documented false-negative source.
- [ ] **Step 5 — run the seven affected suites and commit.**
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b/apps/api && ./vendor/bin/phpunit tests/Feature/Pricing tests/Feature/Taxation tests/Feature/Document/DocumentAdditionalCostTest.php tests/Feature/Identity
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b/apps/api && ./vendor/bin/pint --test database/seeders/ProductionSeeder.php tests/Feature/Pricing tests/Feature/Taxation
      ```
      **`PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest` is in this commit's run list too, and its `INITIALIZATION_ONLY` fixture SHRINKS BY ONE ROW here** — `database/seeders/PermissionSeeder.php::run` leaves with its file. That is the fixture's shrink-only direction working as designed; delete the row in this commit or the partition assertion fails with *"an exempted method that no longer writes must be REMOVED from its fixture"*.
      ```
      git add apps/api/database/seeders/ProductionSeeder.php apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest.php apps/api/tests/Feature/Pricing/CheckMarginTest.php apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/DocumentAdditionalCostTest.php apps/api/tests/Feature/Taxation/TaxConfigurationManagementTest.php apps/api/tests/Feature/Taxation/TaxBreakdownEndpointTest.php apps/api/tests/Feature/Taxation/TaxConfigurationCapabilityDelegationTest.php

## Task 12 (0b-11): the CI `env:` line that switches on the anti-growth direction

This task discharges wave 0a's residual **R-3**. It is a `.github/workflows/ci.yml` edit, and that file is edited by **both** in-flight lanes, which is why it waited for 0b's entry condition.

**R-3 — the anti-growth direction is inert.** `RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest`'s anti-growth case is a `markTestSkipped` until `ROUTE_COVERAGE_BASELINE_PROTECTED_BLOB` is registered. Until then a contributor can add a violation **and** its baseline entry in one commit — the loophole wave 0a's `RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetLivenessTest::matched_growth_defeats_the_checker_which_is_what_the_protected_blob_exists_for` documents executably.

> **R-4 IS ALREADY CLOSED — do not re-do it (wave 0a rev 2, plan gate r1 B-2).** Rev 1 of this plan said the new Architecture classes ran on no CI event and that registering them rode here. Both halves changed: the claim was wrong (they **did** run on `workflow_dispatch`, via the manual full-suite step at `.github/workflows/ci.yml:473-501`; what they did not do was gate PR→`dev`), and wave 0a now registers them itself — **Task 6 step 1 of the 0a plan appends a `backend-architecture` step after `:228` naming all six classes.** This task therefore adds **one `env:` block to that existing step**, not a new step. **Read the step that is actually in the file before editing**; if it is absent, wave 0a did not land its Task 6 step 1 and that is a finding, not a licence to re-create it here with a different class list.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — add the `env:` block to wave 0a's existing step, appended, never a reflow.** Both lanes have hunks elsewhere in this file (W-LOT around `:1130`, T2's PG `--filter` allow-list around `:1138-1145`), so an **append** merges cleanly and a reformat does not. Locate the step wave 0a added after `:228` — `- name: RBAC route-coverage ratchet + self-service + tombstones (wave 0a)` — and give it an `env:` block, mirroring the `backend-dpa-guard` shape at `:313-323`:
      ```yaml
      - name: RBAC route-coverage ratchet + self-service + tombstones (wave 0a)
        env:
          # OWNER-SET repository variable — the anti-growth ceiling, deliberately
          # outside every candidate diff. Unset => the ratchet fails closed, which
          # is the designed forcing function for the owner bootstrap. Same contract
          # as DPA_BASELINE_PROTECTED_BLOB above.
          ROUTE_COVERAGE_BASELINE_PROTECTED_BLOB: ${{ vars.ROUTE_COVERAGE_BASELINE_PROTECTED_BLOB }}
        run: |
          # …the six-class run wave 0a wrote, UNCHANGED. Do not re-list the
          # classes here: if this task and wave 0a disagree about which classes
          # run, the one that gates merges is whichever edit landed last.
      ```
      **Change nothing else in the step**, and add nothing to its `run:` block — the class list is wave 0a's and the zero-selection guard (the house pattern from `:305-311`) is already in it.
- [ ] **Step 2 — replace the ratchet's anti-growth `markTestSkipped`** with the fail-closed-on-unset form `DocumentPerActionBaselineRatchetTest` uses: an unset variable **fails** the test with the bootstrap instruction, rather than skipping.
- [ ] **Step 3 — the repository variable is the OWNER's.** Compute the blob and record it in the handback as an owner-owed item; the test fails closed until the owner sets it, which is the designed forcing function:
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b/apps/api && git hash-object tests/Architecture/baselines/route-permission-coverage-baseline.json
      ```
- [ ] **Step 4 — validate the workflow before committing.** A YAML error in `ci.yml` takes the whole repository's CI down:
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b && actionlint .github/workflows/ci.yml || python3 -c "import yaml,sys;yaml.safe_load(open('.github/workflows/ci.yml'));print('yaml ok')"
      ```
- [ ] **Step 5 — commit.**
      ```
      git add .github/workflows/ci.yml apps/api/tests/Architecture/RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest.php
      ```

## Task 13: the convention-09 rows

Three tests, all asserting **data meaning** — the rows company B sees, the rows written at location B — never HTTP status alone (`docs/conventions/09-SECOND-OF-EVERYTHING.md:52`).

- [ ] **`ServicePermissionsSecondCompanyTest`** — an actor holding `services.update` in company A is refused `PATCH /services/{id}` on a service belonging to company B. Assert the **service row is unchanged**, not merely that the response was 403. Two companies in one tenant, created through the **real registration/company-create path**, not by direct model insert.
- [ ] **`CategoryPermissionsSecondCompanyTest`** — categories are **company**-scoped with no location binding at all (every read and write goes through `companyContext->requireCompanyId()` and a `where('company_id', …)`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/CategoryController.php:28-35,101-108,115-178`), so the axis they actually have is the company one. Assert the **rows returned** per company and that a create in company A does not appear in company B's tree.
- [ ] **`StockAdjustmentLocationScopePermissionTest`** — the second-location axis, on `POST /api/v1/stock-adjustments`. The gate is at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:126-128` (`can:inventory.adjustments.create`) and the **location denial** is enforced at `apps/api/app/Modules/Inventory/Presentation/Requests/StoreStockAdjustmentRequest.php:90` (`new ValidLocationAccess($this->locationContext, $this->companyContext, $company->id)`). Assert that a principal **holding** the permission but restricted by `allowed_location_ids` to location A is refused at location B, and that **removing the restriction lets the identical payload through** — asserting the `stock_adjustments` rows both times.

      > **A prerequisite fact, recorded rather than worked around:** the `ValidateLocationAccess` middleware is registered as `validate.location.access` (`apps/api/bootstrap/app.php:118`) and is attached to **zero routes** in the tree. "A route that enforces the middleware" does not exist to point at; the location enforcement that **is** real on an inventory write is the `ValidLocationAccess` rule and the `LocationContext` guard. A test whose denial never fires proves nothing, which is why this test targets a route where the denial is real.

**Three COMPLETE classes, not a shape plus a substitution table — plan gate r4 MAJOR M4-2.** Rev 4 supplied one abbreviated method, a trait whose last two helpers were in a separate fragment, and a table telling the executor how to derive the other two classes. That is a recipe for three files that do not exist yet, and the stated three-file `phpunit` command could not be verified from it. Rev 5 supplies all three classes in full. **Every request carries `X-Company-Id`** — rev 4's example omitted it "for brevity" and then told the executor not to, which is exactly the instruction that gets dropped.

**The trait first**, `apps/api/tests/Traits/SecondOfEverythingFixtures.php`, because all three classes use it and a fourth convention-09 test in wave 1 will need it again.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Traits;

use App\Modules\Company\Domain\Company;
use App\Modules\Company\Domain\Enums\CompanyStatus;
use App\Modules\Company\Domain\UserCompanyMembership;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\UserStatus;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\User;
use App\Modules\Service\Domain\Service;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Enums\SubscriptionPlan;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Enums\TenantStatus;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Tenant;
use Database\Seeders\RolesAndPermissionsSeeder;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

/**
 * Convention 09 (docs/conventions/09-SECOND-OF-EVERYTHING.md) fixtures.
 *
 * The point of every one of these is that the SECOND thing is made the way a
 * real operator makes it — through the route — because a direct
 * Company::create() skips exactly the code convention 09 exists to exercise.
 */
trait SecondOfEverythingFixtures
{
    /**
     * The FIRST company, memoised so companyForCurrentTenant() is idempotent
     * within one test — registerTwoCompanies() and serviceIn() both reach for
     * it, and a second tenant would silently break every scope assertion.
     */
    private ?Company $currentCompany = null;

    /**
     * Resolved from the container in each using class's setUp():
     * `$this->permissionRegistrar = $this->app->make(PermissionRegistrar::class)`.
     * NEVER the app() helper (CLAUDE.md rule 13). NOT readonly: a PHPUnit
     * TestCase does not own its constructor.
     */
    private PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar;

    /**
     * Two companies in ONE tenant. The first arrives with the tenant; the second
     * is created through POST /api/v1/companies by an actor holding
     * companies.create — the route THIS WAVE gates, which is what makes this
     * helper a test of the wave and not merely a fixture.
     *
     * @return array{0: Company, 1: Company}
     */
    private function registerTwoCompanies(): array
    {
        $companyA = $this->companyForCurrentTenant();
        $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($companyA->tenant_id);
        $this->seed(RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::class);

        $founder = $this->userHolding(['companies.create'], $companyA);

        // SNAKE_CASE, and all four required fields. CreateCompanyRequest::rules()
        // requires name, country_code (size:2), currency (size:3), locale
        // (max:10) and timezone (max:50 + the `timezone` rule) —
        // lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Presentation/Requests/CreateCompanyRequest.php:22-26.
        $response = $this->actingAs($founder)
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyA->id)
            ->postJson('/api/v1/companies', [
                'name' => 'Second company',
                'country_code' => $companyA->country_code,
                'currency' => $companyA->currency,
                'locale' => $companyA->locale,
                'timezone' => $companyA->timezone,
            ]);
        // 201, not 200: CompanyController::store returns
        // response()->json([...], 201) at
        // lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Presentation/Controllers/CompanyController.php:214.
        $response->assertCreated();

        /** @var Company $companyB */
        $companyB = Company::query()->findOrFail($response->json('data.id'));

        // Both directions, asserted HERE so a broken fixture fails as a fixture
        // rather than as a mysterious authorization result three lines later.
        self::assertNotSame($companyA->id, $companyB->id);
        self::assertSame($companyA->tenant_id, $companyB->tenant_id);

        return [$companyA, $companyB];
    }

    /**
     * A user in $company holding EXACTLY the named permission keys — looked up
     * in the seeded catalogue, never firstOrCreate'd. A key that does not exist
     * fails here, loudly, instead of silently producing an actor who is refused
     * for the wrong reason.
     *
     * @param  list<string>  $keys
     */
    private function userHolding(array $keys, Company $company): User
    {
        $user = $this->userForCompany($company);

        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            $permission = Permission::query()
                ->where('name', $key)
                ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
                ->first();

            self::assertNotNull($permission, "permission '{$key}' is not in the seeded catalogue.");

            $user->givePermissionTo($permission);
        }

        return $user->fresh() ?? $user;
    }

    /**
     * One service belonging to $company, created through the service-create
     * route so its company scoping is the route's, not the test's.
     *
     * @param  array<string, mixed>  $attributes
     */
    private function serviceIn(Company $company, array $attributes = []): Service
    {
        $author = $this->userHolding(['services.create'], $company);

        $response = $this->actingAs($author)
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $company->id)
            ->postJson('/api/v1/services', $attributes + [
                'name' => 'B original',
                'pricing_type' => 'fixed',
                'base_price' => '10.000',
            ]);
        $response->assertCreated();

        /** @var Service $service */
        $service = Service::query()->findOrFail($response->json('data.id'));
        self::assertSame($company->id, $service->company_id);

        return $service;
    }

    /**
     * The FIRST company, and the tenant it belongs to. Direct model creation is
     * correct HERE and only here: convention 09's rule is about the SECOND of a
     * thing — registerTwoCompanies() makes that one through the route.
     */
    private function companyForCurrentTenant(): Company
    {
        if (isset($this->currentCompany)) {
            return $this->currentCompany;
        }

        $tenant = Tenant::create([
            'name' => 'Second-of-everything tenant',
            'slug' => 'soe-'.Str::lower(Str::random(8)),
            'status' => TenantStatus::Active,
            'plan' => SubscriptionPlan::Professional,
        ]);

        $this->currentCompany = Company::create([
            'tenant_id' => $tenant->id,
            'name' => 'Company A',
            'legal_name' => 'Company A SARL',
            'tax_id' => 'TAX-SOE-A',
            'country_code' => 'FR',
            'locale' => 'fr_FR',
            'timezone' => 'Europe/Paris',
            'currency' => 'EUR',
            'status' => CompanyStatus::Active,
        ]);

        return $this->currentCompany;
    }

    /**
     * A user in $company's tenant, with an ACTIVE membership in $company. It
     * holds no permission of its own — userHolding() attaches those — and no
     * role, so a test that forgets to grant a key is refused rather than
     * accidentally passing as an admin.
     */
    private function userForCompany(Company $company): User
    {
        $user = User::create([
            'tenant_id' => $company->tenant_id,
            'name' => 'Scoped Actor',
            'email' => 'soe-'.Str::lower(Str::random(10)).'@example.com',
            'password' => 'password123',
            'status' => UserStatus::Active,
        ]);

        UserCompanyMembership::create([
            'user_id' => $user->id,
            'company_id' => $company->id,
            'role' => 'manager',
        ]);

        $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($company->tenant_id);

        return $user;
    }
}
~~~

> **`X-Company-Id` on EVERY request, including the fixture's own.** `CompanyContext::requireCompanyId()` is what the company scope under test reads, and the sibling suite sets it exactly this way (`lane/w-lot-a-1a:apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/CreditNoteTenantIsolationTest.php:516-524`). Rev 4 left it out of its example and added a sentence saying the shipped test must not — an instruction with no code behind it (plan gate r4 **M4-2**). Every `actingAs()` above and in all three classes below carries it.

> **If a sibling suite already has a helper of either name, USE IT and delete the copy.** `tests/Feature/Service`, `tests/Feature/Product` and `tests/Feature/Inventory` each build a company-and-user fixture; if one is already extractable, extract it into this trait rather than shipping a fourth spelling of "make a company", and say in the handback which you reused and which you wrote. The payload fields in `serviceIn()` must match `StoreServiceRequest`'s rules — read it, do not guess, and note that `base_price` is a **string** (rule 19: never a float on money).

**Class 1 — `apps/api/tests/Feature/Service/ServicePermissionsSecondCompanyTest.php`.**

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature\Service;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;
use Tests\TestCase;
use Tests\Traits\SecondOfEverythingFixtures;

/**
 * Convention 09, second-COMPANY axis, on the services.update gate wave 0b adds.
 *
 * The claim is NOT "the permission gate works" — that is Task 6's subject. It is
 * "holding the key is not enough: the company scope still refuses." So the actor
 * KEEPS services.update throughout, and the assertion is on the ROW, never on
 * the status alone (docs/conventions/09-SECOND-OF-EVERYTHING.md:52).
 */
final class ServicePermissionsSecondCompanyTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;
    use SecondOfEverythingFixtures;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->permissionRegistrar = $this->app->make(PermissionRegistrar::class);
    }

    #[Test]
    public function an_actor_scoped_to_a_is_refused_the_same_write_in_b(): void
    {
        // 1. FIXTURE — two of the thing. The second company is created through
        //    the REAL company-create path, never a direct Company::create().
        [$companyA, $companyB] = $this->registerTwoCompanies();
        $actor = $this->userHolding(['services.update'], $companyA);

        // 2. THE ROW BEFORE. Capture the exact column the write would change.
        $service = $this->serviceIn($companyB, ['name' => 'B original']);

        // 3. THE ATTEMPT — PATCH, which is the only verb routes.php:31 defines.
        $response = $this->actingAs($actor)
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyA->id)
            ->patchJson("/api/v1/services/{$service->id}", ['name' => 'A rewrote it']);

        // 4. THE ROW AFTER — the assertion that matters. Status alone would pass
        //    against a route that 500s, one that silently no-ops, and one that
        //    wrote to the wrong company.
        $this->assertDatabaseHas('services', ['id' => $service->id, 'name' => 'B original']);
        $response->assertForbidden();
    }

    #[Test]
    public function the_same_actor_can_patch_a_service_in_its_own_company(): void
    {
        // The positive half, without which the refusal above could be the new
        // GATE rather than the company scope.
        [$companyA] = $this->registerTwoCompanies();
        $actor = $this->userHolding(['services.update'], $companyA);
        $service = $this->serviceIn($companyA, ['name' => 'A original']);

        $response = $this->actingAs($actor)
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyA->id)
            ->patchJson("/api/v1/services/{$service->id}", ['name' => 'A renamed it']);

        $response->assertOk();
        $this->assertDatabaseHas('services', ['id' => $service->id, 'name' => 'A renamed it']);
    }
}
~~~

**Class 2 — `apps/api/tests/Feature/Product/CategoryPermissionsSecondCompanyTest.php`.** Categories are **company**-scoped with no location binding at all (every read and write goes through `companyContext->requireCompanyId()` and a `where('company_id', …)`, `apps/api/app/Modules/Product/Presentation/Controllers/CategoryController.php:28-35,101-108,115-178`), so the company axis is the one they have.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature\Product;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;
use Tests\TestCase;
use Tests\Traits\SecondOfEverythingFixtures;

/**
 * Convention 09, second-COMPANY axis, on the categories.* gates wave 0b adds.
 * Asserted on the returned IDS, not on a count: two companies with one category
 * each have the same count and completely different meaning.
 */
final class CategoryPermissionsSecondCompanyTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;
    use SecondOfEverythingFixtures;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->permissionRegistrar = $this->app->make(PermissionRegistrar::class);
    }

    #[Test]
    public function a_category_created_in_a_is_not_visible_in_bs_tree(): void
    {
        [$companyA, $companyB] = $this->registerTwoCompanies();
        $actor = $this->userHolding(
            ['categories.view', 'categories.create'],
            $companyA,
        );

        // The write, in company A, through the route this wave gates.
        $created = $this->actingAs($actor)
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyA->id)
            ->postJson('/api/v1/categories', ['name' => 'A only', 'parent_id' => null]);
        $created->assertCreated();
        $categoryId = $created->json('data.id');

        // A's tree contains it.
        $treeA = $this->actingAs($actor)
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyA->id)
            ->getJson('/api/v1/categories/tree');
        $treeA->assertOk();
        self::assertContains($categoryId, $this->idsIn($treeA->json('data')));

        // B's tree does NOT — asserted on the ids, and with an actor holding the
        // key in B, so an empty result cannot be a permission artefact.
        $actorB = $this->userHolding(['categories.view'], $companyB);
        $treeB = $this->actingAs($actorB)
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyB->id)
            ->getJson('/api/v1/categories/tree');
        $treeB->assertOk();
        self::assertNotContains($categoryId, $this->idsIn($treeB->json('data')));

        // And the row itself is stamped with A, not B.
        $this->assertDatabaseHas('categories', ['id' => $categoryId, 'company_id' => $companyA->id]);
    }

    /**
     * Flatten a category tree payload to its ids, children included.
     *
     * @param  array<int, array<string, mixed>>  $nodes
     * @return list<int|string>
     */
    private function idsIn(array $nodes): array
    {
        $ids = [];

        foreach ($nodes as $node) {
            $ids[] = $node['id'];

            if (isset($node['children']) && is_array($node['children'])) {
                $ids = array_merge($ids, $this->idsIn($node['children']));
            }
        }

        return $ids;
    }
}
~~~

**Class 3 — `apps/api/tests/Feature/Inventory/StockAdjustmentLocationScopePermissionTest.php`**, the second-LOCATION axis.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature\Inventory;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;
use Tests\TestCase;
use Tests\Traits\SecondOfEverythingFixtures;

/**
 * Convention 09, second-LOCATION axis, on POST /api/v1/stock-adjustments —
 * a route whose permission gate AND location denial both exist today: the gate
 * at Inventory/Presentation/routes.php:126-128 (can:inventory.adjustments.create)
 * and the denial at StoreStockAdjustmentRequest.php:90 (ValidLocationAccess).
 *
 * The ValidateLocationAccess MIDDLEWARE is registered at bootstrap/app.php:118
 * and attached to ZERO routes, so a test pointed at it would prove nothing.
 */
final class StockAdjustmentLocationScopePermissionTest extends TestCase
{
    use RefreshDatabase;
    use SecondOfEverythingFixtures;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->permissionRegistrar = $this->app->make(PermissionRegistrar::class);
    }

    #[Test]
    public function a_principal_restricted_to_location_a_writes_no_row_at_location_b(): void
    {
        [$companyA] = $this->registerTwoCompanies();
        $actor = $this->userHolding(['inventory.adjustments.create'], $companyA);

        // TWO locations in one company — the second-of-everything axis here.
        // Build both through the location-create path the sibling inventory
        // suites already use; read it rather than inserting directly.
        [$locationA, $locationB] = $this->twoLocationsIn($companyA);

        $actor->forceFill(['allowed_location_ids' => [$locationA->id]])->save();

        $payload = $this->adjustmentPayloadAt($locationB, $companyA);

        $refused = $this->actingAs($actor->fresh())
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyA->id)
            ->postJson('/api/v1/stock-adjustments', $payload);

        // The ROW, not the status: a 422 that still wrote is the failure mode
        // a status assertion cannot see.
        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('stock_adjustments', ['location_id' => $locationB->id]);
        self::assertContains($refused->getStatusCode(), [403, 422]);

        // The OTHER direction: remove the restriction, send the IDENTICAL
        // payload, and exactly one row appears. Without this half the test
        // passes against a route that refuses everything.
        $actor->forceFill(['allowed_location_ids' => null])->save();

        $allowed = $this->actingAs($actor->fresh())
            ->withHeader('X-Company-Id', $companyA->id)
            ->postJson('/api/v1/stock-adjustments', $payload);

        $allowed->assertCreated();
        self::assertSame(
            1,
            \DB::table('stock_adjustments')->where('location_id', $locationB->id)->count(),
        );
    }
}
~~~

> **`twoLocationsIn()` and `adjustmentPayloadAt()` are the two helpers this class needs and the trait does NOT supply**, because they are inventory-shaped and the trait is catalogue-shaped. **Read `StoreStockAdjustmentRequest::rules()` and the sibling `tests/Feature/Inventory` suites at the merge moment** and write both as private methods on this class, mirroring whatever the siblings already do to make a location and an adjustment line. `allowed_location_ids` is read by `LocationContext`; confirm the column name and its cast on `User` before writing to it. **If a sibling already has an equivalent helper, use it.** Name what you reused and what you wrote in the handback. This is the one place in Task 13 that is deliberately not supplied verbatim: inventing an adjustment payload from the plan would produce a 422 that looks like a denial, which is the exact failure rev 4's `POST /companies` payload had.

| `CategoryPermissionsSecondCompanyTest` | a second **company** | `GET /api/v1/categories/tree` for company A returns **only** company A's rows, and a `POST /api/v1/categories` in A produces **no** row visible in B's tree — asserted on the returned ids, not the count |
| `StockAdjustmentLocationScopePermissionTest` | a second **location** in one company | with `allowed_location_ids = [A]`, `POST /api/v1/stock-adjustments` at location B writes **no** `stock_adjustments` row; with the restriction removed, the **identical payload** writes exactly one. ## Task 13b (0b-15): the four Identity read-route gates, and the Users/Roles page guards that must land with them

> **Provenance.** Wave 0a's **T3** (the four Identity read-route gates) and **T3b** (the `UsersPage` roles-query guard) were moved here by orchestrator ruling on plan gate r1, **BLOCKER B-3** (`docs/superpowers/reviews/2026-09-10-rbac-plan-0a-codex-gate-r1.md`). The ruling calls this task **"0b-4 read-route gates + Users/Roles page guards"**; `0b-4` is already the accepted spec's id for the `categories.*` key block (plan Task 6, spec §8), so it ships as **0b-15** — the name and the content are the ruling's, only the identifier is disambiguated so no two items share one.

**Why it is here and not in 0a.** Gating `GET /roles` and `GET /permissions` breaks a page a `manager` can open. `RolesPage` fires both reads unconditionally (`dev:apps/web/src/features/settings/RolesPage.tsx:73-80,82-89`; **post-lane `lane/w-lot-a-1a:…:68-75,77-84`**), its route is gated only on `moduleKey="settings"` → `settings.view` (`dev:apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:2344-2351`, `apps/web/src/hooks/usePermissions.ts:97`), and its Settings card carries no permission check at all — `SettingsSection` has no permission field (`apps/web/src/features/settings/SettingsPage.tsx:23-53`). `manager` holds `settings.view` (`dev:apps/web/src/hooks/permissionsMap.generated.ts:254`; **post-lane `:255`, where the lane also adds `general_manager` to it**) but neither `roles.view` nor `users.assign-roles`, both `admin`-only (`dev:243,272`; **post-lane `:244,274`**; seeder `dev:apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:379,382`, **post-lane `:459,462`**). Gating the API without gating the page ships a guaranteed double 403 on load and the loading-error surface (`RolesPage.tsx:246-249`). The fix needs `RolesPage.tsx`, `routes/index.tsx` and `usePermissions.ts` — **all three in `lane/w-lot-a-1a`'s diff** — which is exactly what wave 0b's entry condition already waits for. Sequencing cost: zero.

**Today's defect this closes (I-18).** `GET /api/v1/users/{userId}/roles` has **no gate at all** and returns **any tenant user's full `getAllPermissions()` list to any authenticated caller** (`RoleController::userRoles`, payload at `apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php:429-437`). `GET /roles`, `GET /roles/{id}` and `GET /permissions` are equally ungated, while the comments above them at `apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/routes.php:58` (*"Role management (requires roles.view or roles.manage permission)"*) and `:77` (*"User role assignment (requires users.assign-roles permission)"*) are false statements. This task makes them true.

**Scope boundary — gating and page guards now, PAYLOAD SHAPING in wave 2a.** Owner ruling D4 gives a bare `users.assign-roles` holder role **names**, not the permission matrix, and spec §4.6.4 specifies three payload shapes for it. That shaping changes `RoleController`'s response builders and is a **wave-2a** deliverable (spec §8). This task closes the *"any authenticated caller"* half and leaves the *"an assign-roles holder still sees the matrix"* half open — residual **R-0b-8** below.

**Files**

- Modify: `apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/routes.php:58-64,77-78` — the four read gates.
- Modify: `apps/api/tests/Architecture/RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest.php` — add `IDENTITY_READ_CLOSURES` + its assertion; `UNCOVERED_READ_CEILING` **146 → 142**.
- Modify: `apps/api/tests/Architecture/baselines/route-permission-coverage-baseline.json` — delete four entries (298 → 294 before this wave's own closures).
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/settings/UsersPage.tsx:136` — gate the roles query's `enabled` on `users.assign-roles`.
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/settings/__tests__/UsersPage.tenantScope.test.tsx:85` — the fixture user gains `admin` so the roles-key assertion at `:175` still exercises an **enabled** query.
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/settings/UsersPage.test.tsx` — two guard cases.
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/settings/RolesPage.tsx` — both queries gated on `roles.view`. `dev:73-80,82-89`; **post-lane-merge `:68-75,77-84`** — the numbers an executing agent will actually see (plan gate r2 **minor 4**; rev 2 gave only the `dev` range even though it said the lane citations were re-measured. The register's own lane range, `:68-83`, is one line short: the second query's closing `})` is at `:84`).
- Modify: `apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx` — the `roles` route gains `permission="roles.view"` beside its `moduleKey="settings"`. `dev:2343-2352` (guard `:2346`); **post-lane-merge `:2350-2359` (guard `:2353`)** — the number an executing agent will actually see.
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/settings/SettingsPage.tsx:23-53,64-75` — `SettingsSection` gains an optional `permission`, the Roles card declares `roles.view`, and the grid filters on it.
- Create: `apps/web/e2e/rbac-role-read-gates.spec.ts` — the Playwright spec `AGENTS.md:12-16` requires for UI work.
- Modify: `apps/web/src/hooks/usePermissions.ts` — **expected UNTOUCHED.** It is listed because the ruling names it and because `lane/w-lot-a-1a` edits it: if the Roles card or route turns out to need a `MODULE_PERMISSIONS` entry, that is where it goes, and the overlap must be re-checked at that moment. `roles.view` is already in the generated map (`dev:permissionsMap.generated.ts:243`, **post-lane `:244`**), so `hasPermission('roles.view')` needs no change here — unlike Task 6's eighteen keys, which are in neither union until Task 6 Step 3(f) regenerates the file.

**Interfaces**

- Consumes `RouteCoverageClassifier`, `RouteCoverage` and `RoutePermissionCoverageScanner` from wave 0a Task 2.
- Produces `RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest::IDENTITY_READ_CLOSURES: array<string, string>` — route key ⇒ the gate expression it must carry.
- No new permission key, no seeder edit, no migration. Every key already exists.

**The two keys are not interchangeable (owner ruling D4; restated at wave-0a plan gate r2 M-5).** Read this before writing any guard in this task:

| Surface | Guard key | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `UsersPage`'s `/roles` query (`UsersPage.tsx:136`) | **`users.assign-roles`** | The page's job is assigning roles. `GET /roles` deliberately accepts `roles.view` **or** `users.assign-roles` (spec `:1610,1616-1622`), so guarding this query on `roles.view` would hide the role-name list from exactly the actor the any-of gate exists for. |
| Both `RolesPage` queries, the `/settings/roles` route, the Settings Roles card | **`roles.view`** | These surface the permission **matrix**, which is what `roles.view` names. `GET /permissions` and `GET /roles/{id}` are `can:roles.view` only. |

**Gate mapping (spec §4.6.4, owner ruling D4).** Both keys are in `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::permissionNames()` at `:379` (`users.assign-roles`) and `:382` (`roles.view`) — **not** `:382-383`; `:383` is `roles.manage` (gate r2 citation audit).

| Route | Line at base | Gate |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/roles` | `apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/routes.php:59` | `require.any.permission:roles.view,users.assign-roles` — a role **assigner** must be able to list role names |
| `GET /api/v1/roles/{id}` | `:61` | `can:roles.view` — the detail carries the permission set |
| `GET /api/v1/permissions` | `:64` | `can:roles.view` |
| `GET /api/v1/users/{userId}/roles` | `:78` | `require.any.permission:roles.view,users.assign-roles` |

The **five** write routes in those blocks — `POST /roles` (`:60`), `PATCH /roles/{id}` (`:62`), `DELETE /roles/{id}` (`:63`), `POST` and `DELETE /users/{userId}/roles` (`:79,80`) — are **left alone**: they are checked in `RoleController` today and their conversion to route gates is wave 3's consistency sweep. (Rev 1 of the 0a plan said "three write routes" and then named five — gate r1 m-3.)

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — the backend red.** In `apps/api/tests/Architecture/RoutePermissionCoverageRatchetTest.php`, add the constant and the test:

~~~php
    /**
     * 0b-15, spec 4.6.4 (owner ruling D4). Four reads that are ungated today; the
     * worst of them, GET users/{userId}/roles, returns any tenant user's full
     * getAllPermissions() list to ANY authenticated caller (I-18).
     *
     * Every key already exists in the seeded catalogue — this task adds none.
     *
     * PAYLOAD SHAPING IS WAVE 2a, NOT HERE: D4 gives a bare `users.assign-roles`
     * holder role NAMES, not the matrix, and that lives in RoleController's
     * response builders. This task closes the "any authenticated caller" half;
     * residual R-0b-8 carries the rest.
     *
     * @var array<string, string>
     */
    public const IDENTITY_READ_CLOSURES = [
        'GET api/v1/roles' => 'require.any.permission:roles.view,users.assign-roles',
        'GET api/v1/roles/{id}' => 'can:roles.view',
        'GET api/v1/permissions' => 'can:roles.view',
        'GET api/v1/users/{userId}/roles' => 'require.any.permission:roles.view,users.assign-roles',
    ];

    #[Test]
    public function the_identity_role_reads_carry_their_declared_gate(): void
    {
        $classifier = new RouteCoverageClassifier;

        foreach (self::IDENTITY_READ_CLOSURES as $key => $expected) {
            $route = $this->findRoute($key);

            self::assertSame(
                RouteCoverage::Gated,
                $classifier->classify($route),
                $key.' is not gated. Spec 4.6.4 requires '.$expected.'.',
            );
            self::assertContains(
                $expected,
                array_values(array_filter(
                    $route->gatherMiddleware(),
                    static fn (string|object $middleware): bool => is_string($middleware),
                )),
                $key.' is gated, but not with the declared expression '.$expected.'.',
            );
        }
    }

      Phase 0.2.15: Gate the four role/permission read routes and the pages that call them (I-18)

      GET /users/{userId}/roles returned ANY tenant user's full
      getAllPermissions() list to ANY authenticated caller (RoleController.php

        GET roles/{id}             can:roles.view
        GET permissions            can:roles.view
        GET users/{userId}/roles   require.any.permission:roles.view,users.assign-roles

      users.view) reaches BOTH Settings -> Users and Settings -> Roles. Without
      the guards these gates would 403 on every manager page load: UsersPage fired
      GET /roles unconditionally, and RolesPage fires GET /roles AND
      GET /permissions with no permission check, behind a route gated only on

          leads to a page its viewer will be refused.

      in the handback with its cause; a ceiling is never adjusted to match a hope.

      textually merged: two lanes raise it in the same window and a textual merge of
      two ceilings is arithmetic nobody checked.

      scripts/permissions-ensure-0b.sh runs both permissions:ensure-fleet invocations
      under `set -euo pipefail` and is the ONLY writer of
      /var/run/autoerp/permissions-ensure.status: `ok` if and only if both exited 0,
      else `failed:<which>`. The command itself writes no status file — no single
      exit code can express a two-invocation deploy. Wave 1 deletes this script in
      the same commit that deletes the commands it wraps.
